PURPOSE OF THIS MASTER FILE
---------------------------
This file combines the mapping notes produced across the Player-Sect mod lineage and the later
V60 reverse-engineering passes. The front section is the CURRENT corrected interpretation.
Older reports are preserved verbatim in the chronological archive even when a later pass corrected
them. When an older appendix conflicts with this front section, the latest correction ledger wins.

====================================================================================================
A. CURRENT MASTER MODEL — LATEST CORRECTED INTERPRETATION
====================================================================================================

A1. PLAYER-SECT SCOPE / SCHOOL IDENTITY
---------------------------------------
- The intended scope remains the NPC's exact CURRENT Player School/Sect, not a broad top-school
  family scope.
- Persistent School identity is DataUnit.UnitInfoData.schoolID at runtime +0x30.
- WorldUnitData._school is a runtime cache at +0x38.
- WorldUnitData.get_school @ 0x056478DC resolves persistent schoolID and may refresh that cache.
- The current is_player_school_npc helper compares the resolved current School to the Player School.
- Child branches are therefore not automatically treated as current Player-Sect members after
  relocation unless their actual current School identity resolves to the Player School.
- SchoolJoin / SchoolExit and relocation/direct-schoolID paths were mapped separately because they
  can change membership or leave UnitPower cache state stale.

A2. RUNUNIT MONTHLY ATTEMPT BUDGET
---------------------------------
- WorldUnitAIBase.RunUnit @ 0x0560A15C uses a monthly quota stored at RunUnit closure/object +0x20.
- The current Aggressive Catch-up V2 helper is FC-safe and does NOT call the unsafe progression-state
  helper from global RunUnit.
- For selected Player-Sect NPCs, the current effective pre-RNG floor is:
      max(19, native config minimum)
- CommonTool.Random integer upper bound is treated as EXCLUSIVE in the corrected mapping; therefore
  Random(W21, W21+1) returns exactly W21.
- The +0x20 value is an ATTEMPT quota, not a count of successful progression actions.
- One quota unit can be consumed before a normal goal is actually created.

A3. OUTER GOAL SELECTION — REAL EFFECTIVE WEIGHT
------------------------------------------------
- V16/V17 resolved the goal score semantically:
      effectiveGoalWeight = ConfNpcGoalBaseItem.weight + WorldUnitAIGoalBase.weightAdd
- The low-level candidate form is:
      candidate.+0x2C + candidate.+0x18->+0x28
- The first float sort is ASCENDING, then npcGoalMaxWeightCount front-trims the low end and keeps the
  highest K candidates.
- After merge/eligibility stages, a second sort is DESCENDING.
- Native integer weighted-selection helpers use the same effective score family.
- Therefore large progression weights are not cosmetic: they feed real native weighted selection.

A4. CURRENT PROGRESSION WEIGHT MODIFIERS
----------------------------------------
- Action1031 cultivation:
    Smart Phase priority can become 60000 only while the NPC actually NEEDS EXP.
    Once capped, it falls back to native weight so phase/major-breakthrough paths can compete.
- Goal101 / phase progression:
    weightAdd at +0x2C is multiplied x20 for exact current Player-Sect and directly contributes to
    effectiveGoalWeight.
- Major-breakthrough material action:
    WorldUnitAIAction1051.UpdateWeightAdd is the mapped +0x10 x20 family used by the Goal141 path.
- Goal141 target-route construction:
    familyWeight, friendWeight, enemyWeight remain native;
    gridWeight is x20 for exact current Player-Sect.
- Grid/material x20 therefore increases preference for the grid route, but does not zero the native
  social routes.

A5. SMART PHASE STATE MACHINE
-----------------------------
Current custom progression states:
  0 = unknown/end
  1 = NEED_EXP
  2 = MINOR_CAPPED
  3 = MAJOR_CAPPED

Behavior:
- Action1031 gets 60000 only for NEED_EXP.
- Action1029, when MINOR_CAPPED, can move an exact already-owned vanilla phase item to the front of
  the existing candidate list; it does not inject items.
- Action1004, when MINOR_CAPPED, can prefer an exact phase item among already-valid market
  candidates; it does not inject stock or bypass price/eligibility.
- Realm/Grade remains native; there is no direct realm write.

A6. BOUTEXHAUSTED — IMPORTANT INTERCEPT BEFORE CREATEGOAL
---------------------------------------------------------
V17 corrected the old interpretation of the late Random(0,10000)<+0x80 loop.
It is NOT the progression success gate.

Exact type:
  WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted

Fields:
  +0x80 = rate
  +0x88 = key/log key

Flow:
  weighted normal goal already selected
      -> GetEffects<WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted>()
      -> for each active effect: Random(0,10000) < rate ?
         no  -> normal WorldUnitAIBase.CreateGoal continues
         yes -> WorldUnitLogMgr.AddLogData(effect.key), callback/end path; normal goal is skipped
                for that attempt.

V19 found exactly four V60 payload literals:
  boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90010022
  boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90020011
  boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90020021
  boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90020031

All encode rate=5000 => 50% intercept chance PER ACTIVE BoutExhausted effect.
This does NOT mean every NPC permanently loses 50% of attempts; the effect must be attached/active.
The numeric families correlate strongly with postnatal feature families 9001002 and 9002001/2/3,
but the exact live feature-row -> effect-row attachment remains not fully closed.

A7. GOAL101 BREAKTHROUGH RNG
----------------------------
- WorldUnitAIGoal101.UpGrade contains passSufferingProb.
- It is a RESCUE/SECOND-CHANCE roll after UnitActionRoleUpGrade has already entered failure state 2.
- Exact current Player-Sect ordinary UnitActionRoleUpGrade.GetCompleteRate is overridden to 100.
- With Random(0,100) producing 0..99, the ordinary mapped Player-Sect upgrade normally enters state 1
  directly, so passSufferingProb is normally moot.
- Do NOT treat passSufferingProb as the base breakthrough-success chance.
- UnitActionRoleUpGradeRule and UnitActionRoleUpGradeElder use separate special-upgrade logic and are
  NOT proven to use the same ordinary 100% completion helper.

A8. GOAL141 -> ACTION1051 -> SEEKUPGRADE — DIRECT MAJOR-BREAKTHROUGH PATH
-------------------------------------------------------------------------
Current exact family mapping:
  Goal141 -> Action1051 -> UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade
  Goal142 -> Action1052 -> UnitActionRoleHelpSeekProp
  Action1053 -> UnitActionRoleJealous
  Action1054 -> UnitActionRoleTeachSkill / Goal144 family
  Action1055 -> Goal145-style reward/item logic

Thus Action1052–1055 are not hidden alternate Goal141 branches.

Goal141 target-route keys:
  0_ = family
  1_ = friend
  2_ = enemy
  3_ = grid

Goal141 serializes the chosen target unitID into customActionData; Action1051 resolves that fixed
unit and constructs UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.

A9. SEEKUPGRADE TWO-GATE MODEL — CURRENT STRONGEST DELAY
--------------------------------------------------------
UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.NPCToNPCAction has at least two important gates:

  Gate 1: target/cooperation completion
    - remains native
    - probability is affected by native factors such as base rate, relative grades, beauty,
      intimacy/family relationship and Action1051-related modifiers.
    - if this gate fails, the later dungeon gate is never reached.

  Gate 2: dungeon completion
    - current exact Player-Sect helper forces threshold 100 once this stage is reached.

Therefore the strongest currently mapped ordinary major-breakthrough delay is Gate 1, not the
already-patched dungeon gate.

A10. seekGradeNeedNum=1 — V19 CORRECTION
----------------------------------------
V18 temporarily described +0x158 as a failed-seek counter. V19 corrected this.

For the mapped ordinary NPC->NPC isUpGrade route:
- OnEnd only constructs/invokes its progression callback after:
      isTargetComplete == true AND isDungeonComplete == true
- that callback increments WorldUnitAIDataBase +0x158.
- first-gate failures do NOT increment +0x158.

So +0x158 is best described as a QUALIFYING SUCCESSFUL-SEEK progression count on this route.
Current seekGradeNeedNum=1 means that after the first qualifying successful seek, native
AddUpGradeProps can already become eligible to create/reward a configured required material and
continue the upgrade flow.

Implication:
- reducing seekGradeNeedNum below 1 is meaningless;
- raising quotas or dungeon success cannot rescue attempts that fail Gate 1;
- the most valuable next probability audit is the Gate-1 cooperation formula.

A11. CURRENT PATCH/BEHAVIOR INTENT
----------------------------------
The current progression philosophy remains:
- exact current Player-Sect only;
- improve opportunity/priority, not direct Realm/Grade writes;
- use native material IDs and native item systems;
- no free market stock injection;
- CostPropItem remains native;
- Goal142 otherwise native unless separately mapped;
- family/friend/enemy weights remain native;
- avoid global RNG patches and global +0x80 patches;
- keep crash safety above aggressive forcing.

====================================================================================================
B. CORRECTION / SUPERSESSION LEDGER
====================================================================================================

Use these latest corrections when reading the chronological archive below:

1. SORT DIRECTION
   Correct:
     flag/W2=0 -> DESCENDING
     flag/W2=1 -> ASCENDING
   Any preliminary statement saying the reverse is superseded.

2. npcGoalMaxWeightCount
   It is a TOP-K preselection limiter after ascending sort; it trims low scores from the front and
   keeps the highest K selector/effective-score candidates. It is not the monthly action quota.

3. LATE +0x80 / +0x88 LOOP
   Superseded theory: final progression candidate success gate.
   Correct: WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted rate/key processing.

4. STRUCTURAL +0x80 WRITER @ 0x0555EC98
   Superseded use: candidate identity example for RunUnit final list.
   Correct owner: WorldUnitEffectSpecialEquipSkill.Create; matching offsets were coincidental across
   special-effect subclasses.

5. 0x055C21D4 RNG
   Superseded: another Goal101 RNG.
   Correct: WorldUnitAIGoal102.UpdateWeightAdd using lookBookBasicsProbability.

6. 0x055BAE84
   Earlier shorthand often called it Goal141 major-breakthrough priority.
   Exact owner: WorldUnitAIAction1051.UpdateWeightAdd. It is still part of the Goal141 progression
   family, but it is action-layer weight, not Goal141's own outer goal field.

7. seekGradeNeedNum / +0x158
   Superseded V18 wording: failed-seek counter.
   Correct V19 wording: qualifying successful-seek progression count in the mapped NPC->NPC route.

8. Action1052–1055
   Superseded concern: possible hidden Goal141 alternatives.
   Correct: separate goal/action families; Goal141's direct material path is Action1051.

9. Goal101 passSufferingProb
   Superseded concern: base breakthrough chance.
   Correct: rescue roll after ordinary breakthrough failure state.

10. CommonTool.Random int bounds
    Current corrected model uses max-exclusive semantics for the relevant mapped integer overload.
    Any older note treating Random(W21,W21+1) as W21 or W21+1 is superseded.

11. SchoolData.GetPostUnit(0)
    Corrected mapping: aggregate formal post groups 1–6, NOT a reserve/no-post pool.

====================================================================================================
C. CURRENT DO-NOT-PATCH LIST
====================================================================================================

Until the remaining V20 targets are closed, do NOT blindly:
- raise RunUnit quota above 19 to hide failed attempts;
- increase existing 60000/x20 progression multipliers further;
- force all +0x80 fields to 10000;
- disable all special effects globally;
- patch passSufferingProb to 100;
- zero family/friend/enemy Goal141 route weights globally;
- force Gate-1 cooperation success globally;
- claim Rule/Elder special upgrade classes are already covered by ordinary GetCompleteRate=100;
- direct-write NPC Realm/Grade.

====================================================================================================
D. BEST NEXT MAPPING BASELINE (V20)
====================================================================================================

Priority 1 — first target/cooperation gate formula
  Fully name every config field and modifier in UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.NPCToNPCAction Gate 1,
  quantify its final threshold, and determine a safe Player-Sect-only minimum if needed.

Priority 2 — BoutExhausted attachment sources
  Recover exact feature/effect rows linking 9001002 / 9002001 / 9002002 / 9002003 to the four
  boutExhausted payloads and estimate how often ordinary Player-Sect NPCs can carry them.

Priority 3 — special upgrade classes
  Map UnitActionRoleUpGradeRule and UnitActionRoleUpGradeElder success/failure probability paths and
  determine whether they need the same Player-Sect completion treatment as ordinary UpGrade.

Priority 4 — runtime validation
  If safe telemetry/logging is possible, measure how often selected Player-Sect progression attempts
  are lost specifically to BoutExhausted versus Gate-1 cooperation failure.

9. V52B SECT STAGE A — RECRUITMENT INFRASTRUCTURE [STATIC-ONLY]
===============================================================================

A. Target successful named Outer recruitment = 7 untuk PLAYER Sect
------------------------------------------------------------------
Hook caller:
- VA 0x05157630
- BL -> helper v37_hook_outyear_count VA 0x08C784C8

Target constant current:
- helper VA 0x08C784F8
- MOV W0,#7

Behavior:
- Player Sect: return 7
- Other Sect: original GetOutYearCount()

KOREKSI HISTORI:
- Catatan V51A lama menyebut monthly/system-end cycle. Mapping lanjutan menunjukkan gate
  automatic Outer intake vanilla berjalan tahunan (month % 12 == 0).
- V52B BELUM mengubah gate tahunan menjadi 6 bulan.
- Angka 7 adalah target successful join; hasil aktual dapat <7 jika kandidat valid kurang.

B. Named Outer Disciple cap = 40 untuk PLAYER Sect
---------------------------------------------------
Hook site:
- VA 0x05157690
- original: LDR W28,[X0,#0x28] (post numMax)
- V52B: branch -> helper VA 0x08C78618

Helper:
- cek School melalui filter player-Sect yang sudah dipakai jalur V37.
- Player Sect -> W28=40
- Other Sect -> load numMax vanilla.
- return ke VA 0x05157694.

C. Named Inner Disciple cap = 20 untuk PLAYER Sect
---------------------------------------------------
Hook site:
- VA 0x0513FB00
- original: LDR W9,[X0,#0x28]
- V52B: branch -> helper VA 0x08C7864C

Helper:
- Player Sect -> W9=20
- Other Sect -> numMax vanilla.
- return ke VA 0x0513FB04.

FILTER AUTO-RECRUIT YANG MASIH VANILLA:
- kandidat merupakan NPC dunia yang sudah ada.
- bukan Player.
- belum punya Sect.
- Realm/grade harus lolos area compatibility.
- join cooldown/history masih dicek.
- UnitActionSchoolJoin tetap menentukan valid/tidak.
- kandidat valid diacak; V52B TIDAK sort berdasarkan ATK/power.

AKIBAT:
- 7 tidak menjamin 7 jika slot/kandidat/syarat tidak cukup.
- Cap 40/20 mengurangi bottleneck named-post, tetapi tidak bypass UnitActionSchoolJoin.

===============================================================================
10. V52B PLUGIN NOTICE NARROW HIDE FIX [STATIC-ONLY]
===============================================================================

Masalah V52A:
- PluginMiddleware.CheckLoadCache VA 0x05681ADC dibuat RET.
- Ini menghilangkan notice tetapi juga memotong cache/init/callback.
- Runtime: klik New Save tidak lanjut ke Difficulty.

V52B:
1) Restore CheckLoadCache penuh
   VA          : 0x05681ADC
   file offset : 0x0567DADC
   V52A        : RET
   V52B        : original SUB SP,SP,#0xB0 prologue + body normal.

2) Skip HANYA block UI detected-plugin notice
   VA          : 0x0568213C
   file offset : 0x0567E13C
   Vanilla     : TBZ W0,#0,0x0568229C
   V52B        : B 0x0568229C

Effect yang diharapkan:
- cache plugin tetap dibangun.
- plugin forced-On tetap aktif.
- continuation callback tetap jalan.
- New Save -> Difficulty kembali normal.
- block pembuatan "Active plugins detected" dilewati.

BELUM DIANGGAP STABLE sampai perangkat mengonfirmasi keempat hal:
1. New Save membuka Difficulty.
2. Popup "Active plugins detected" tidak muncul.
3. minimal satu forced plugin masih bekerja.
4. save lama/Sect masih dapat dibuka.

===============================================================================
11. FITUR YANG SUDAH DIMAPPING TAPI BELUM MASUK V52B [PLANNED]
===============================================================================

JANGAN mengira fitur berikut sudah aktif:

A. Elite recruit +50% sekali saja
- Rencana titik aman: post-join callback SchoolEffectUnitAttr.OnUnitActionSchoolJoin().
- Marker persistence kandidat: joinSchoolsID agar bonus tidak stack saat rejoin.
- BELUM masuk V52B.

B. Recruitment 6 bulan
- Vanilla gate saat ini tetap annual/month%12.
- Rencana hanya mengubah gate untuk Player Sect setelah target 7 terbukti stabil.
- BELUM masuk V52B.

C. Faster breakthrough-material seeking
- Mapping menunjukkan seekGradeNum / seekGradeNeedNum dan native AddUpGradeProps/RoleSeekUpGrade.
- Rencana aman: effective seekGradeNeedNum <=2 khusus Player-Sect NPC.
- BELUM masuk V52B.

D. Better NPC manual intelligence
- schoolBuyFailProb menggunakan skala persen 0-100.
- schoolNextGradeProb menggunakan skala persen 0-100.
- Rencana awal: fail kira-kira 5%, next-grade preference kira-kira 80%.
- BELUM masuk V52B.

E. Promotion speed
- Native promotion menggunakan UnitPower.TotalPower / GetUnitPower dan
  UnitActionSchoolSetPostType.
- Rencana hanya memperpendek interval Elder/Grand Elder; Successor/Sect Master tetap vanilla.
- BELUM masuk V52B.

F. Natural leave reduction
- DelOutUnit juga terhubung ke maintenance/promotion; tidak boleh di-RET.
- Belum diubah. Capacity test dilakukan lebih dahulu.

G. PlayerPlugins #69 SectBattleResurrection
- Mapping: death veto khusus SchoolWar.SchoolWarUnitDie; tidak generic immortality.
- BELUM forced-On di V52B.

4. Pastikan max named Outer dapat melewati cap vanilla lama hingga target 40.

STOP TEST JIKA:
- save slot tidak lanjut.
- Sect menu hilang/tidak bisa diklik.
- named members kosong mendadak.
- Manual Library kembali kosong.
- FC pada world entry/recruitment tick.

V52B — HAL YANG PERLU DIPERHATIKAN / ROLLBACK GUIDE
====================================================

1) SAFE ANCHOR
- Jika V52B gagal karena notice/Sect Stage A, basis paling aman untuk gameplay lama adalah V41.
- Jika ingin plugin pack tetapi tanpa Stage A, V50 adalah referensi.
- V52A tidak boleh dijadikan basis karena CheckLoadCache broad RET memutus New Save flow.

2) JIKA NEW SAVE TIDAK MASUK DIFFICULTY
- Curigai patch PluginMiddleware.CheckLoadCache / notice flow terlebih dahulu.
- V52B harus mempunyai original method di VA 0x05681ADC dan narrow branch di 0x0568213C.
- Jangan RET seluruh CheckLoadCache.

3) JIKA SECT RUSAK / MENU TIDAK BISA DIKLIK
- Jangan menyentuh OnSystemEnd1 atau memanggil AddOutUnit sendiri.
- Cek bahwa hanya count wrapper 0x05157630 + cap hooks 0x05157690/0x0513FB00 yang berubah.
- Lima eksperimen V37 lain harus tetap rollback.

4) JIKA NAMED DISCIPLE TIDAK MENJADI 7
Ini belum tentu bug.
- annual recruitment event mungkin belum terjadi.
- candidate pool dapat kekurangan NPC tanpa Sect/grade area valid.
- join cooldown/history dapat menolak kandidat.
- 7 adalah target successful join, bukan forced spawn.
- Outer cap 40 hanya membuka kursi; tidak menciptakan kandidat.

5) JIKA MANUAL LIBRARY / MARKET ANEH
- Pastikan V48/V49 Market Fist/Palm patch tidak ikut tersalin.
- Pastikan 12 V41 manual generator rollback sites tetap seperti V35 source-load behavior.
- Jangan aktifkan #67 SecretBookType saat diagnosis manual.

6) PLUGIN SHADOW
- V52B menyembunyikan System Notice saja.
- Plugin tetap enabled secara internal.
- Jika status/toggle masih terlihat di menu plugin, itu masalah UI-state berbeda; jangan
  mematikan cache plugin untuk menyembunyikannya.

7) SAVE SAFETY
- Jangan tes ReviveNPC pada unique/story NPC terlebih dahulu.
- Jangan direct-write Realm, Sect ID, or post/rank.
- Buat backup save sebelum annual Sect recruitment tick pertama V52B.

8) NEXT BUILD PRINCIPLE
- Satu subsystem per build test.
- Stage B = elite +50% one-time post-join.
- Stage C = cultivation/material/manual intelligence.
- Stage D = promotion + SectWar resurrection.

TALE OF IMMORTAL ARM64 — RENCANA MOD BERIKUTNYA + HASIL MAPPING LOKASI DETAIL
================================================================================
Build referensi : V52B_SECT_STAGE_A_PLUGIN_NOTICE_NARROW_HIDE_FIX_TEST
Tanggal         : 2026-08-27
Arsitektur      : ARM64 / IL2CPP
Tujuan dokumen  : menjadi catatan kerja untuk mod Sect berikutnya, BUKAN daftar fitur
                  yang semuanya sudah aktif.

PENTING — CARA MEMBACA STATUS
-----------------------------
[ACTIVE-V52B]       = sudah ada di libil2cpp.so V52B.
[MAPPED-SAFE]       = alur native dan fungsi target sudah terpetakan cukup baik, tetapi patch belum dipasang.
[MAPPED-PARTIAL]    = subsistem sudah ditemukan, tetapi masih perlu pemilihan caller/branch paling sempit.
[DO-NOT-TOUCH]      = pernah terbukti berisiko atau mapping menunjukkan lokasi tersebut bukan target yang benar.
[RUNTIME-TEST-REQ]  = secara statik masuk akal, tetapi wajib diuji Android sebelum dianggap stabil.

Dokumen ini sengaja memisahkan tiga hal:
1) apa yang SUDAH aktif di V52B;
2) apa yang INGIN kita pasang pada Stage B/C/D;
3) apa yang sudah diperiksa tetapi sengaja TIDAK direkomendasikan.

================================================================================
A. TARGET BESAR MOD SECT YANG KITA INGIN CAPAI
================================================================================

Target akhir bukan sekadar "spawn banyak NPC", tetapi membuat Sect player berkembang melalui
jalur native game:

World NPC vanilla
  -> automatic Outer recruitment vanilla
  -> sampai 7 successful named recruits pada event recruitment
  -> post-join callback native
  -> bonus elite satu kali
  -> cultivation/catch-up cepat
  -> AI mencari breakthrough material native
  -> UnitActionRoleUpGrade vanilla + success 100%
  -> NPC membeli/study/equip/train manual berkualitas tinggi
  -> native TotalPower menentukan promosi
  -> anggota penting dilindungi dari permanent death saat Sect War

PRINSIP KESELAMATAN:
- Jangan direct-write School ID.
- Jangan direct-write Realm/Grade.
- Jangan direct-write post/rank.
- Jangan memasukkan NPC langsung ke member list.
- Jangan memanggil AddOutUnit sendiri dari luar cadence vanilla.
- Jangan mematikan DelOutUnit secara keseluruhan.
- Gunakan action/callback native sebanyak mungkin.

================================================================================
B. STAGE A — RECRUITMENT INFRASTRUCTURE [ACTIVE-V52B]
================================================================================

1. Gate waktu automatic Outer recruitment
-----------------------------------------
Fungsi:
  MapBuildSchoolPostOut.OnSystemEnd1()
RVA/VA yang telah dipetakan:
  sekitar 0x05157114

HASIL PEMERIKSAAN:
- Fungsi ini menjalankan system-end Sect dan memiliki gate berdasarkan waktu dunia.
- Gate vanilla efektif lolos pada kelipatan 12 bulan; jadi automatic Outer recruitment
  pada jalur ini bersifat tahunan, bukan recruitment pasti setiap bulan.
- V37 pernah mengambil alih urutan OnSystemEnd1 dan memaksa AddOutUnit; itu menyebabkan
  state Sect bermasalah dan kemudian di-rollback pada V40/V41.

KEADAAN V52B:
- OnSystemEnd1 tetap vanilla.
- Cadence tetap 12 bulan.

RENCANA:
- Jangan ubah cadence sebelum target 7 + named-cap terbukti stabil beberapa cycle.
- Bila Stage A stabil, opsi aman berikutnya adalah mengubah gate player-Sect saja menjadi
  6 bulan, bukan memanggil AddOutUnit tambahan.

DILARANG:
- Jangan kembali ke forced AddOutUnit call.
- Jangan suppress call AddOutUnit vanilla.


2. Automatic recruitment utama
-------------------------------
Fungsi:
  MapBuildSchoolPostOut.AddOutUnit()RVA:
  sekitar 0x05157410

HASIL PEMERIKSAAN:
- Mengambil NPC dunia yang sudah ada; bukan membuat NPC baru secara paksa.
- Kandidat harus lolos filter vanilla, termasuk status Sect dan compatibility grade/area.
- Kandidat valid kemudian diacak/shuffle; vanilla tidak melakukan sorting ATK tertinggi.
- Join dilakukan lewat UnitActionSchoolJoin, bukan raw School ID write.
- Karena kandidat berasal dari dunia, target 7 adalah target successful join; jika kandidat
  valid sedikit, hasil aktual masih dapat <7.

CIRI UnitActionSchoolJoin yang dipakai auto Outer recruitment yang sudah dipetakan:
- type/post target = 6 (Outer Disciple)
- isTest = true
- isCheckExitMonth = true
- isPlayerRecruit = false

Field/history terkait yang ditemukan:
- joinSchoolsID
- lastExitSchoolMonth
- join/rejoin eligibility history

KEADAAN V52B:
- AddOutUnit tetap vanilla.
- Tidak ada +50% stat hook di dalam loop AddOutUnit.


3. Target successful recruit
-----------------------------
Call-site sempit yang dipakai V51A/V52B:
  VA 0x05157630
Helper custom:
  0x08C784C8
Target constant current:
  0x08C784F8 -> 7

KEADAAN V52B:
- Player Sect -> target 7.
- Sect lain -> vanilla.

CATATAN:
- Angka 7 bukan forced-spawn 7 NPC.
- Kandidat dapat ditolak oleh grade/area, current Sect, join history/cooldown, atau cap post.


4. Named Outer Disciple cap
----------------------------
Hook:
  0x05157690
Custom helper:
  0x08C78618
Current target:
  40 untuk player Sect

HASIL PEMERIKSAAN:
- Named Outer mempunyai cap post terpisah dari backup/total population.
- Helper total/backup member minimum ~1000 dari build lama tidak berarti Outer named cap=1000.

KEADAAN V52B:
- player-Sect Outer cap = 40.
- Sect lain tetap vanilla.


5. Named Inner Disciple cap
----------------------------
Hook:
  0x0513FB00
Custom helper:
  0x08C7864C
Current target:
  20 untuk player Sect

KEADAAN V52B:
- player-Sect Inner cap = 20.
- Sect lain tetap vanilla.

REKOMENDASI CAP FINAL:
- Outer 40 sudah masuk range aman yang kita inginkan (30–50).
- Inner 20 sudah masuk range aman yang kita inginkan (15–30).
- Jangan menaikkan named NPC ke ratusan/1000 karena AI/save simulation cost.

================================================================================
C. MAPPING YANG SALAH SASARAN DAN SUDAH DIKOREKSI [DO-NOT-TOUCH]
================================================================================

1. outNPCAddRate1 / outNPCAddRate2 / outNPCAddRate3
---------------------------------------------------
HASIL AKHIR PEMERIKSAAN:
- Integer relative weights, bukan 0–100 success chance.
- Digunakan oleh:
    MapBuildSchoolPostOut.GetBattleUnits()
  RVA sekitar:
    0x05159340
- Menentukan kategori/komposisi NPC trial/battle Outer, BUKAN kualitas automatic recruit.

Konsekuensi:
- 1/1/20 berarti ~90.9% weight kategori 3 pada subsystem tersebut, tetapi tidak membuat
  automatic recruit memilih disciple lebih kuat.
- Jangan mod rate1/2/3 untuk target auto-recruit elite.

2. outNPCMaxCount
-----------------
- Berkaitan dengan pool/count NPC pada jalur battle/trial yang sama.
- Bukan named auto-recruit capacity utama.

3. outRadius1 / outRadius2
--------------------------
HASIL PEMERIKSAAN:
- Dipakai oleh Area10GridCreateTool.SplitArea10 / pembagian world-map/grid.
- Bukan radius pencarian kandidat Sect.

DILARANG:
- Jangan ubah outRadius1/2 untuk recruitment; berisiko mengganggu world generation/grid.

================================================================================
D. STAGE B — ELITE DISCIPLE ONE-TIME BONUS [MAPPED-SAFE, BELUM AKTIF]
================================================================================

TUJUAN:
- NPC yang sukses masuk sebagai automatic Outer Disciple player Sect menerima bonus baseline
  sekali saja.
- Tidak diberikan ulang jika NPC keluar lalu bergabung lagi.

TITIK POST-JOIN NATIVE YANG DITEMUKAN:
  SchoolEffectUnitAttr.OnUnitActionSchoolJoin()
RVA:
  sekitar 0x0513AC70

KENAPA TITIK INI LEBIH AMAN:
- Callback terjadi setelah game memproses UnitActionSchoolJoin.
- Callback mengambil action melalui get_action() dan memvalidasi bahwa action benar-benar
  UnitActionSchoolJoin.
- Callback juga dapat memeriksa School yang terkait.
- Kita tidak perlu lagi menulis bonus di dalam AddOutUnit loop seperti V37.

PEMBEDA AUTO-RECRUIT YANG DIREKOMENDASIKAN:
- action type/post = Outer / 6
- isTest = true
- isCheckExitMonth = true
- isPlayerRecruit = false
- School == player School
- actor != player

MARKER "SEKALI SAJA":
- joinSchoolsID menyimpan School yang pernah dimasuki NPC.
- Rencana aman: hanya beri bonus jika current player School belum tercatat pada history
  first-join tersebut.
- Harus dipastikan patch ditempatkan pada sisi callback/timing yang membaca history dengan
  benar agar bonus tidak stack saat rejoin.

BONUS YANG DIREKOMENDASIKAN:
- HP / MP / SP             +50%
- Attack / Defense         +50%
- Crit / Crit Damage       +50%
- Guard / GuardValue       +50%
- Martial RES              +50%
- Spiritual RES            +50%
- 6 Martial aptitudes      +50%
- 6 Spiritual Roots        +50%
- 6 Artisanship            +50%
- Health / Energy          +50%
- Immortal Point           +50%

JANGAN DIUBAH:
- NPC ID
- age
- lifespan
- personality
- relationship
- story/quest flags
- Taoist identity/state
- School ID
- post/rank
- Realm/Grade

ALASAN:
- NPC tetap NPC dunia asli dan semua identity/story state tetap native.
- Bonus stat akan meningkatkan TotalPower secara natural dan membantu promosi kemudian.

STATUS IMPLEMENTASI:
- Fungsi/callback sudah ditemukan.
- Exact branch/cave final untuk V53/Stage B belum dipasang.
- V37 old hook 0x051577CC TIDAK BOLEH dipakai kembali.

================================================================================
E. STAGE C1 — CULTIVATION CATCH-UP [SEBAGIAN ACTIVE, UPGRADE PLANNED]
================================================================================

CURRENT V52B (inherited stable branch):
- player-Sect NPC same/normal           x5 EXP
- 1 grade tertinggal                    x8
- 2 grade tertinggal                    x12
- >=3 grade tertinggal                  x15

BREAKTHROUGH SUCCESS:
- 100% setelah NPC sudah eligible dan benar-benar melakukan attempt.

CURRENT NPC BREAKTHROUGH STAT GROWTH:
- ATK             x2.5
- DEF             x4
- HP/MP/SP        x2
- Crit            x2
- Crit Damage     x5
- Guard/GuardVal  x4
- Martial RES     x4
- Spiritual RES   x4
- Immortal Point  x2

REKOMENDASI NANTI SETELAH STAGE B STABIL:
- normal/sama        x5
- tertinggal 1       x10
- tertinggal 2       x15
- tertinggal >=3     x25

CATATAN:
- Jangan naikkan ke x50 sebelum material gate selesai, karena EXP penuh sendiri tidak menjamin
  NPC dapat melakukan breakthrough.

================================================================================
F. STAGE C2 — BREAKTHROUGH MATERIAL AI [MAPPED-SAFE, BELUM AKTIF]
================================================================================

PIPELINE NATIVE YANG SUDAH DITEMUKAN:
EXP penuh
 -> AI goal upgrade
 -> cek requirement/material
 -> trouble/seek action bila kurang
 -> memperoleh material native
 -> UnitActionRoleUpGrade
 -> CostPropItem
 -> breakthrough
 -> success roll

CLASS/FUNGSI YANG SUDAH DIPERIKSA:
- WorldUnitAIGoal101
  Memutuskan kebutuhan/eligibility mengejar breakthrough.

- UnitTrouble101 / UnitTrouble102
  State trouble ketika requirement/material belum terpenuhi.

- UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade
  Action mencari requirement breakthrough.

- AddUpGradeProps()
  Helper native yang memberikan prop/material yang sesuai requirement.

- NpcCountMgr.AddUpGradeProps()
- NpcCountMgr.UpGrade()
  Jalur native lain untuk menyiapkan material lalu melakukan upgrade.

- WorldUnitAIAction1051
  Terhubung ke seek-upgrade progression.

- WorldUnitAIGoal141
  Memakai counter/threshold:
    seekGradeNum
    seekGradeNeedNum

- WorldUnitAIGoal142.CheckNeedProp()
  Menghitung kekurangan material/requirement dan membandingkan kebutuhan low material.

- UnitActionRoleUpGrade.OnCreate()
  Melakukan jalur upgrade normal dan mengonsumsi item menggunakan CostPropItem.

HASIL PEMERIKSAAN KRITIS:
- Success 100% bukan bottleneck utama bila NPC tidak punya material.
- Game sudah punya mekanisme memberi material yang benar; tidak perlu fabricate arbitrary item ID.

REKOMENDASI PATCH:
1) Khusus NPC anggota player Sect.
2) Effective seekGradeNeedNum = min(vanilla, 2).
3) Pertahankan UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade dan UnitActionRoleUpGrade native.
4) Jika masih lambat setelah runtime test, baru pertimbangkan weight/frequency seek action x2.

JANGAN:
- direct-write Realm/Grade.
- skip IsCreate/eligibility seluruhnya.
- memanggil AddUpGradeProps setiap bulan tanpa gate.
- memberi semua breakthrough props globally.

STATUS:
- subsistem sudah terpetakan;
- caller/branch paling sempit player-Sect-only masih perlu dipilih saat implementasi Stage C.

================================================================================
G. STAGE C3 — NPC MANUAL INTELLIGENCE [MAPPED-SAFE, BELUM AKTIF]
================================================================================

FUNGSI UTAMA:
  UnitActionNpcBuyBook.OnCreate()
RVA:
  sekitar 0x0546E2B8

CONFIG YANG SUDAH DITEMUKAN:
  ConfNpcBuyBookBase

FIELD PENTING:
- schoolBuyFailProb
- schoolCostEnergyMin
- schoolCostEnergyMax
- schoolNextGradeProb
- schoolThisGradeLevelMin
- schoolThisGradeLevelMax
- schoolNextGradeLevelMin
- schoolNextGradeLevelMax

SKALA PROBABILITY SUDAH DIPASTIKAN:
- schoolBuyFailProb memakai Random(0,100) < value.
  0   = gate ini tidak membuat gagal.
  5   ~= 5% fail.
  100 = selalu gagal pada gate tersebut.

- schoolNextGradeProb juga skala 0–100.
  80 ~= 80% memilih jalur next-grade ketika valid.

ENERGY COST:
- schoolCostEnergyMin/Max digunakan sebagai persentase personal Energy NPC;
- bukan langsung menguras Sect Money.

JALUR MANUAL NATIVE YANG DITEMUKAN:
- UnitActionMartialEquip
- UnitActionMartialRealize
- UnitActionMartialTrain
- UnitActionPropMartialStudy

HASIL PEMERIKSAAN:
- NPC mempertimbangkan aptitude/build/manual yang sedang dipakai.
- Tidak perlu raw manual ID injection.

REKOMENDASI TARGET AWAL:
- effective schoolBuyFailProb = 5 untuk NPC player-Sect.
- effective schoolNextGradeProb = 80.
- level min/max digeser ke upper valid range, bukan dipaksa ke grade invalid.
- energy cost tetap moderat agar AI tidak menjadi free/infinite action.

PERHATIAN:
- Jangan gunakan #67 SecretBookType saat mendiagnosis sistem manual ini; plugin tersebut menyentuh
  subsystem manual type dan dapat menyulitkan isolasi masalah.

STATUS:
- logic/scale sudah jelas;

Tale of Immortal ARM64 — V50 Plugin Pack + Market Rollback
==========================================================

BASE
- Built directly from V41 MANUAL_LIBRARY_SAFE_ROLLBACK_TEST.
- V48/V49 Town Manual/Market Fist-Palm injection is NOT included.
- V42-V46 Main Martial Arts/Sect experiments are NOT included.
- Therefore Town Manual Pavilion/Market follows the V41 safe path again.

PLUGINS FORCED ON AT NATIVE INITIALIZATION
Ordinal numbering follows the 87 PlayerPlugins list discovered in metadata.

#1  AnimaWeaponAllUsePlugin         internal plugin ID 70
#2  AnimaWeaponQuickRepairPlugin    internal plugin ID 72
#17 ForcedRecruitPlugin             internal plugin ID 75
#34 MartialRealizePerfectPlugin     internal plugin ID 58
#35 MatirailUpPlugin                internal plugin ID 53
#44 MultipleSpiritFavorPlugin       internal plugin ID 11
#51 PotmonGiveUpTrait               internal plugin ID 52
#60 ReviveNPCPlugin                 internal plugin ID 86

ACTIVATION METHOD
Each requested plugin is redirected at the end of its Initialize method from
PluginBase.Initialize (saved toggle state) to PluginBase.OnEnable so it is
explicitly enabled in this build. #1 was previously hard-routed to OnDisable
in V41; V50 explicitly changes it to OnEnable.

PATCH SITES (VA)
#1  0x568522C  B  -> PluginBase.OnEnable 0x5680708
#2  0x56855F0  B  -> PluginBase.OnEnable 0x5680708
#17 0x568A3C8  B  -> PluginBase.OnEnable 0x5680708
#34 0x56FEAF4  B  -> PluginBase.OnEnable 0x5680708
#35 0x56FEB10  B  -> PluginBase.OnEnable 0x5680708
#44 0x5702314  BL -> PluginBase.OnEnable 0x5680708
#51 0x570667C  B  -> PluginBase.OnEnable 0x5680708
#60 0x5708248  B  -> PluginBase.OnEnable 0x5680708

#35 MATIRAILUPPLUGIN — WHAT IT ACTUALLY UPGRADES
- This is NOT a generic ore/herb/crafting-material upgrader.
- It adds a Martial Arts/manual Grade Upgrade option in UIMartialInfo.
- GetTargetPropData clones the target manual and raises MartialData.grade by +1.
- Required materials are data-driven per target manual/current-target grade.
- The UI builds a needCostProps list containing item ID + required count.
- IsHasCostProps checks those exact item IDs/counts in player inventory.
- MaterialUpGrade/BeginUpGrade consumes the required props via CostPropItem.
- Therefore there is no single universal material family; cost varies according
  to the manual and grade chosen by the game's own configuration.

#17 FORCEDRECRUITPLUGIN — BEHAVIOR
- Internal plugin ID: 75.
- FixData itself is a RET; the game checks PluginIsEnabled(75) directly inside
  UnitActionFeedback115.OnCreate.
- When the forced-recruit path is active, it relaxes normal recruitment gates.
- The observed flow can bypass later Heaven-chosen/Taoist-heart and relative
  power-ratio eligibility checks when its friend/intimacy-side condition passes.
- It does NOT directly insert an NPC into the Sect member/disciple list.
- It does NOT by itself shorten a monthly recruitment timer/cooldown.
- V41 did not hard-force this plugin ON; V50 does.

#60 REVIVE NPC CAUTION
- ReviveNPCPlugin exposes revive functionality in relation/NPC UI paths.
- Use selectively on ordinary NPCs first. Reviving story/unique NPCs may create
  quest-state inconsistencies if the story expects that NPC to remain dead.

VALIDATION
- Same libil2cpp.so size as V41.
- Exactly 8 ARM64 call/branch instructions changed.
- No Market/TownManual generator code added.
- No Sect basTypes/Main Martial Arts code added.

Tale of Immortal ARM64 — V51A Player Sect 3 Named Disciple Safe Count Test
==========================================================================

BASE
- Built directly from V50 MARKET_ROLLBACK_PLUGIN_PACK_TEST.
- All V50 forced-on stealth plugins are retained: #1, #2, #17, #34, #35, #44, #51, #60.
- V48/V49 Market Fist/Palm changes remain excluded.
- V42-V46 Main Martial Arts experiments remain excluded.

PURPOSE
- Increase the vanilla named-disciple recruitment target for the PLAYER'S Sect to 3 per PostOut/system-end recruitment cycle.
- Preserve the vanilla Sect state flow that recovered in V40/V41.

SAFE APPROACH
- MapBuildSchoolPostOut.OnSystemEnd1 remains vanilla.
- Both vanilla AddOutUnit calls remain vanilla; neither is suppressed or forced.
- The old V37 new-recruit +50% join-result hook remains OFF.
- Only the existing V37 GetOutYearCount wrapper is reused because it already contains a runtime player-Sect identity filter.
- For the player's Sect the wrapper returns 3.
- For other Sects it calls the original GetOutYearCount unchanged.

PATCHES VS V50
1) VA 0x05157630 / file 0x05153630
   Original V50: BL original GetOutYearCount
   V51A:         BL v37_hook_outyear_count at VA 0x08C784C8

2) Custom helper file offset 0x08A404F8
   MOV W0,#2 -> MOV W0,#3

CYCLE CLARIFICATION
- This is the PostOut / Sect system-end recruitment cycle used by the game's periodic Sect update.
- Prior V37 mapping identified it as the monthly/system-end recruitment cycle, not a once-per-year wait.
- 3 is a target/attempt count. Actual successful named recruits may be fewer if vanilla candidate/eligibility logic finds fewer valid NPCs.

REALM PROGRESSION RETAINED FROM V41/V50
- Player-Sect NPC cultivation EXP: x5 baseline.
- Catch-up: x8 if 1 grade behind, x12 if 2 grades behind, x15 if >=3 grades behind.
- Breakthrough complete rate: 100% after the NPC reaches an eligible breakthrough attempt.
- Player-Sect breakthrough stat growth multipliers remain active.
- Other Sect NPCs remain vanilla through the player-Sect runtime filter.

NOT INCLUDED YET
- No +50% stat bonus for newly recruited NPCs in this build.
- No change to breakthrough eligibility gates.
- No change to Elder/Grand Elder assignment.
- No change to Backup Disciple generation/mortality.

VALIDATION
- ELF remains ELF64 AArch64 DYN.
- Exactly 2 ARM64 instructions changed versus V50 (5 byte positions differ because MOV #2 -> #3 changes one byte).
- Recruitment call at VA 0x05157630 disassembles to BL 0x08C784C8.
- V50 plugin activation sites are untouched.
- Runtime Android test is still required.

Tale of Immortal ARM64 — V52A Sect Stage A + Plugin Notice Hidden Test
======================================================================

BASE
- Built directly from V51A PLAYER_SECT_3_DISCIPLE_SAFE_COUNT_TEST.
- Inherits V50 forced-active plugin pack (#1,#2,#17,#34,#35,#44,#51,#60).
- Keeps V41-safe Market/Manual path; V48/V49 Town Manual Fist/Palm experiments remain excluded.
- Keeps V42-V46 Main Martial Arts experiments excluded.

A) SECT STAGE A — RECRUITMENT INFRASTRUCTURE ONLY
1. Player-Sect named Outer recruitment target: 3 -> 7.
   - Existing V37/V51 player-Sect identity-filtered GetOutYearCount helper is retained.
   - Player Sect returns 7; other Sects call vanilla GetOutYearCount.
   - Patch: custom helper VA 0x08C784F8 MOV W0,#7.

2. Player-Sect Outer Disciple named capacity (post 6): 40.
   - Hook site VA 0x05157690, original LDR W28,[X0,#0x28].
   - Redirected to helper VA 0x08C78618.
   - Helper checks MapBuildSchoolPostOut.school with the proven v37_is_player_school filter.
   - Player Sect: W28=40.
   - Other Sects: original numMax loaded unchanged.
   - Returns to VA 0x05157694; the rest of AddOutUnit remains vanilla.

3. Player-Sect Inner Disciple named capacity (post 5): 20.
   - Hook site VA 0x0513FB00, original LDR W9,[X0,#0x28].
   - Redirected to helper VA 0x08C7864C.
   - Player Sect: W9=20.
   - Other Sects: original numMax loaded unchanged.
   - Returns to VA 0x0513FB04; native Inner promotion logic remains intact.

NOT CHANGED IN STAGE A
- No forced OnSystemEnd1 call.
- No suppression of vanilla AddOutUnit calls.
- No +50% stat bonus yet.
- No faster 6-month recruitment yet; current vanilla yearly gate is retained for this first 7-target test.
- No manual-quality patch.
- No breakthrough-material AI patch.
- No promotion-rank field writes.
- No DelOutUnit/leave suppression.
- No #69 SectBattleResurrection forced activation yet.

B) PLUGIN DETECTION NOTICE HIDDEN
Problem observed at runtime:
- Opening/loading the plugin UI displayed a System Notice beginning with
  "Active plugins detected:" and listed the forced-active plugins.

Mapping result:
- PluginMiddleware.CheckLoadCache (VA 0x05681ADC) is the active-plugin load/cache
  detection path. Its body calls PluginMiddleware.GetPluginsTitle twice while
  constructing the detected-plugin title list.

V52A patch:
- VA 0x05681ADC: method entry replaced with RET.
- Result: the active-plugin load/cache System Notice path is skipped.
- The eight V50 plugin OnEnable redirects are untouched, so their internal effects
  remain enabled.
- This patch hides the detection NOTICE. It does not remove the plugin classes or
  rewrite their gameplay functionality.

CUSTOM RX SEGMENT
- Previous: file 0x08A40000 / VA 0x08C78000, size 0x618.
- V52A: extended safely to size 0x680 to hold two player-Sect capacity helpers.
- New helper code is appended at the previous end; existing custom hooks/data are unchanged.

STATIC VALIDATION
- ELF64 AArch64 DYN remains valid.
- Custom RX PT_LOAD remains R-X, align 0x4000.
- Outer hook decodes to B 0x08C78618.
- Inner hook decodes to B 0x08C7864C.
- CheckLoadCache entry is RET.
- Recruit target helper is MOV W0,#7.
- All eight V50 forced-plugin activation instructions are byte-identical to V51A.
- Common-file differences vs V51A are limited to:
  * PT_LOAD filesz/memsz extension,
  * Outer cap hook branch,
  * Inner cap hook branch,
  * CheckLoadCache RET,
  * recruit target 3->7,
  * plus 104 appended helper bytes.

RECOMMENDED TEST ORDER
1. Launch/load the same save used with V51A.
2. Open the plugin screen / reproduce the point that previously showed
   "Active plugins detected". Confirm the System Notice no longer appears.
3. Confirm at least one forced plugin effect still works (e.g. artifact repair,
   Perfect Comprehension, Force Recruitment, or Revive NPC UI path).
4. Open the Sect and confirm all Sect screens still work normally.
5. Advance to the next vanilla annual PostOut recruitment event.
6. Compare named Outer Disciple count before/after. Up to 7 successful joins are targeted,
   but actual joins can still be lower if vanilla candidate/eligibility filters lack candidates.
7. Check Inner/Outer lists and promotion screens for normal behavior.
8. Do NOT judge the later +50%/manual/breakthrough plans from this build; they are not included yet.

RUNTIME STATUS
- Static validation only. Android runtime confirmation is required before Stage B.

Tale of Immortal ARM64 — V52B Sect Stage A + Narrow Plugin Notice Hide Fix
============================================================================

BASE
- Direct correction of V52A_SECT_STAGE_A_PLUGIN_NOTICE_HIDDEN_TEST.
- Keeps all Sect Stage A changes from V52A:
  * Player-Sect Outer recruitment target = 7.
  * Player-Sect Outer named capacity = 40.
  * Player-Sect Inner named capacity = 20.
  * Other Sects remain vanilla through player-Sect filters.
  * OnSystemEnd1 and AddOutUnit remain vanilla.
- Keeps the V50 forced-active plugin pack (#1,#2,#17,#34,#35,#44,#51,#60).

RUNTIME REGRESSION IN V52A
- V52A replaced PluginMiddleware.CheckLoadCache entry with RET to hide
  the "Active plugins detected" System Notice.
- Runtime test showed New Save slots could be clicked, but the game no longer
  advanced to Difficulty selection.
- Root cause: CheckLoadCache also builds/initializes plugin cache/state and
  participates in the continuation callback used by the new-save flow.
  Returning from the entire method was therefore too broad.

V52B FIX
1) Restore CheckLoadCache completely.
   - VA 0x05681ADC / file 0x0567DADC
   - V52A: RET
   - V52B: original SUB SP,SP,#0xB0 prologue, so the complete normal method runs.

2) Hide only the final detected-plugin notice block.
   - At VA 0x0568213C / file 0x0567E13C the vanilla code tests whether the
     active-plugin title/result list requires the System Notice.
   - Original instruction:
       TBZ W0,#0,0x0568229C
     If the result is false/empty it already skips the notice and executes the
     continuation callback.
   - V52B replaces only this instruction with:
       B 0x0568229C
   - Therefore cache initialization, title collection, plugin state, and
     continuation logic still run; only the UI-notice construction block
     0x05682140..0x05682298 is bypassed.

EXPECTED RESULT
- New Save -> Difficulty flow should work again like V51A/V50.
- Forced plugin effects remain active.
- "Active plugins detected" System Notice should not be rendered.
- Sect Stage A remains unchanged from V52A.

UNCHANGED
- No +50% elite recruit stats yet.
- No 6-month recruitment frequency patch.
- No manual-quality or breakthrough-material AI changes.
- No promotion-speed changes.
- No #69 forced activation yet.

TEST ORDER
1. Launch game and press a New Save slot.
2. Confirm Difficulty selection opens normally.
3. Continue far enough to confirm no "Active plugins detected" System Notice appears.
4. Confirm at least one forced plugin effect still works.
5. Open/load a Sect save and verify Sect menus are normal.
6. For Stage A, test the next vanilla annual Outer recruitment event separately.

RUNTIME STATUS
- Static ARM64 validation complete; Android runtime confirmation required.

    0x0546E884 -> next-grade handler

TALE OF IMMORTAL ANDROID ARM64 — V60 SMART PHASE + AGGRESSIVE CATCH-UP FC FIX V2
=================================================================================
Date: 2026-08-31
Status: TEST BUILD — static verification PASS, Android runtime test required

WHY V1 FC'D / WHAT V2 CHANGES
-----------------------------
Aggressive V1 FC'd immediately on Next Month. Smart Phase AI itself had already run successfully,
so the new RunUnit scheduler was isolated as the regression area.

V1 called get_progression_state() from WorldUnitAIBase.RunUnit for essentially every eligible
Player-Sect requester. That helper was validated inside progression-specific actions
(Action1031/1004/1029), where progression data is already in the expected context. V2 removes that
call entirely from RunUnit so the global scheduler no longer dereferences progression dynamic data.

0x05225F48 is NOT removed: re-audit confirms native Action1031 itself calls it using the exact chain
Global root 0x026A9074 -> [root+0x20] -> BL 0x05225F48. The month getter chain is native-proven.

FC FIX V2 COHORT
----------------
RunUnit hook: VA 0x0560A5E8 -> helper 0x08C78BA0.
The displaced SDIV W8,W20,W22 is replayed.

Scope remains current Player-Sect NPC via existing is_player_school_npc @ 0x08C78080.
Outside current Player Sect remains vanilla.

Selection using the already-live native realm gap:
  gap >= 2 : 100% selected
  gap == 1 : ~50% selected/month
  gap == 0 : ~25% selected/month

Same-realm ~25% means roughly 5 selected from 20 eligible NPCs. The selected subset rotates with
native current month using a lightweight requester-pointer hash.

Selected NPCs receive downstream pre-RNG budget 19; native inclusive Random(19,20) remains intact,
so their final Goal/opportunity budget is 19 or 20.

UNCHANGED SMART PHASE AI
------------------------
Action1031 smart cultivation stop: unchanged.
Action1029 exact owned phase-item priority: unchanged.
Action1004 exact market phase-item priority: unchanged.
No direct Realm/Grade writes, free phase items, or forced breakthrough are added.

ELF / BINARY
------------
Smart Phase custom RX: 0xBA0
V2 helper append      : 0x90
New custom RX         : 0xC30
Flags                 : R-X
Alignment             : 0x4000

Within the previous Smart Phase lib prefix, only the custom PT_LOAD p_filesz/p_memsz fields and the
single RunUnit hook are changed. The 0x90-byte helper is appended exactly at the previous EOF.

FIRST RUNTIME TEST
------------------
Use the same save that FC'd with Aggressive V1 and advance exactly ONE month first.
If that works, advance another 2-3 months and count how many different current-Sect NPCs progress.

If V2 still FCs exactly on Next Month, remove only the RunUnit aggressive scheduler and keep the
already-stable Smart Phase AI.

PATCH V60
---------
1) Refill/reseed cadence lebih cepat
   WorldRunMap.OnGridMainThread @ VA 0x056674EC
   V59: CMP W0,#4
   V60: CMP W0,#2

   Geomancy/Medicinal/Mineral tetap vanilla 100/0.

   Anima-only reseed branch selesai menghitung vanillaResult di W1.

     W1 = W1 * 3
     jika W1 < 300 -> 300
     jika W1 > 400 -> 400
     BL GridData.SetAnimaValue @ 0x040B1DF8
     kembali ke native 0x05668038
   Ini hanya berlaku ketika RNG sudah memilih Anima untuk spot baru/reseed.

=======================================================================
1. ROOT CAUSE UTAMA: REALM-GAP MENGURANGI MONTHLY GOAL BUDGET NPC
=======================================================================

WorldUnitAIBase.RunUnit:
  VA 0x0560A15C

Relevant ConfGameParameter getters:
  npcGoalMin             0x032F9DC0
  npcGoalMax             0x032F9E18
  npcGoalMinClamp        0x032F9E70
  npcGoalMaxClamp        0x032F9EC8
  npcGoalSub             0x032F9F20
  npcGoalMaxWeightCount  0x032F9F78

Requester/NPC grade getter:
  WorldUnitDynData.GetGrade 0x0563CFA8

Core code:
  0x0560A4AC  get_npcGoalMin
  0x0560A4B8  MOV W21,W0                 ; base goal count

  0x0560A500  requester GetGrade -> W22
  0x0560A530  player GetGrade    -> W23
  0x0560A55C  SUB  W20,W22,W23
  0x0560A57C  CNEG W20,W20,MI            ; abs(NPC grade - Player grade)

  0x0560A598  get_npcGoalSub
  0x0560A5A4  MOV W22,W0

  0x0560A5E8  SDIV W8,W20,W22            ; floor(abs(gap)/npcGoalSub)
  0x0560A5F0  SUB  W20,W21,W8            ; base - realm-gap penalty

  0x0560A5F4  get_npcGoalMinClamp
  0x0560A600  CMP W20,W0
  0x0560A604  CSEL W21,W20,W0,GT         ; max(base-penalty,minClamp)

  0x0560A648  ADD W1,W21,#1
  0x0560A64C  MOV W0,W21
  0x0560A658  CommonTool.Random
  0x0560A660  store final monthly goal count

High-confidence effective formula in this caller:

  monthlyGoalBudget =
    max(
      npcGoalMin - floor(abs(NPC_grade - Player_grade) / npcGoalSub),
      npcGoalMinClamp
    )

Important V60 observation:
  npcGoalMax is read and converted at 0x0560A4D8..0x0560A4E0,
  tetapi nilai hasilnya tidak dibawa ke jalur final count di atas.
  Jadi pada jalur final RunUnit V60 yang dipetakan, npcGoalMin-lah base yang
  benar-benar dipakai untuk perhitungan tersebut.

Conclusion:
  NPC yang realm/grade-nya jauh tertinggal dari player mendapat lebih sedikit
  kesempatan Goal per siklus bulanan. Ini terjadi SEBELUM multiplier V58
  (cultivation/Goal101/Goal141) bisa membantu.

Confidence: VERY HIGH / binary-confirmed.

Candidate future patch site (BELUM DIPATCH):
  VA          0x0560A5E8
  File offset 0x056065E8
  Original    SDIV W8,W20,W22
  Bytes       88 0E D6 1A

Safe concept:
  non Player-Sect NPC -> native SDIV
  Player-Sect NPC     -> W8 = 0 (tidak kena realm-gap inactivity penalty)

Native SUB/clamp/random/goal-selection tetap dipertahankan.
Ini BUKAN unlimited actions.

=======================================================================
2. SECOND-STAGE BOTTLENECK: npcGoalMaxWeightCount
=======================================================================

Getter:
  ConfGameParameterBase.get_npcGoalMaxWeightCount 0x032F9F78

Use around:
  0x0560C970 get_npcGoalMaxWeightCount
  0x0560C99C weighted/list selection path

Interpretation:
  Ada dua lapisan berbeda:

  A) RunUnit monthly goal budget
     -> berapa banyak Goal yang boleh dieksekusi NPC.

  B) weighted candidate selection
     -> berapa candidate Goal berbobot yang masuk/select pada tahap berikutnya.

Implikasi:
  Goal101/Goal141 weight x20 hanya membantu kompetisi pada lapisan pemilihan.
  Weight besar TIDAK mengembalikan monthly slots yang sudah hilang akibat
  realm-gap penalty pada lapisan A.

Confidence: HIGH.

=======================================================================
3. ACTION1031 CULTIVATION — lastTrainsMonst CONFIRMED
=======================================================================

WorldUnitAIAction1031.UpdateWeightAdd:
  hook V58 at VA 0x055AFA8C
  file          0x055ABA8C
  -> custom helper 0x08C78680

Current custom helper behavior:
  - replay native final MADD
  - compare currentMonth vs lastTrainsMonst
  - require current Player-Sect NPC
  - if currentMonth > lastTrainsMonst => final weight = 60000
  - otherwise native result

WorldUnitAIDataBase.Data field:
  lastTrainsMonst = +0x5C

Generated wrapper evidence:
  GetWrapper_lastTrainsMonst RVA 0x057E6B34
  getter body resolves Data +0x5C
  setter writes Data +0x5C

Important:
  Action1031 patch hanya menaikkan PRIORITY/weight.
  Ia tidak langsung mengeksekusi cultivation,
  tidak menulis EXP/Realm,
  dan tidak bypass native action creation.

Risk diagnosis:
  Ketika monthly goal budget NPC sudah kecil akibat realm gap,
  cultivation weight 60000 dapat terlalu dominan dan mengambil kesempatan yang
  seharusnya bisa dipakai Goal101/Goal141.

Confidence: HIGH.

=======================================================================
4. fullExpMonth — EXACT OFFSET DAN EXACT SEMANTICS
=======================================================================

WorldUnitAIDataBase.Data:
  fullExpMonth = +0x10

Generated wrapper:
  GetWrapper_fullExpMonth RVA 0x057E5A54
  body resolves Data +0x10

Native lifecycle mapped in:
  WorldUnitAIDataBase.<OnSystem>b__11_0
  RVA 0x0560E83C

Relevant flow:
  0x0560E8F4  ConfRoleGradeBase.GetItem
  0x0560E8FC  LDR W8,[gradeItem,#0x28]    ; phase
  0x0560E900  CMP W8,#1

If phase == 1 (Early/new realm):
  0x0560E90C  get_data
  0x0560E914  STR WZR,[Data,#0x10]
              => fullExpMonth = 0

Then if fullExpMonth is still 0:
  0x0560E91C  get_data
  0x0560E924  LDR W8,[Data,#0x10]
  0x0560E928  CBNZ => do not rewrite existing timestamp

  0x0560E964  ConfRoleGrade.IsUpGrade
  if true:
    0x0560E9A4  WorldRunMgr.get_roundMonth
    0x0560E9AC  STR W0,[Data,#0x10]
                => fullExpMonth = current roundMonth

ConfRoleGrade.IsUpGrade:
  RVA 0x0405BB04

Exact key checks:
  0x0405BBD0  LDR W8,[currentGradeItem,#0x28]
  0x0405BBD4  CMP W8,#3
              => requires phase == 3 (Late)

  0x0405BBC4  GetNextGradeItem
  0x0405BBF4  LDR W19,[nextGradeItem,#0x48]
              => next grade EXP requirement

  current unit EXP is read via DynInt.get_value
  0x0405BC04  CMP currentExp, nextRequirement
  true if currentExp >= nextRequirement

ConfRoleGradeItem field mapping confirms:
  +0x28 = phase
  +0x48 = exp

Therefore fullExpMonth semantics are now HIGH CONFIDENCE:

  fullExpMonth == 0
      NPC belum tercatat dalam major-breakthrough-ready full-EXP state.

  fullExpMonth != 0
      NPC berada/sempat masuk kondisi:
        current phase == Late
        AND current EXP >= next realm EXP requirement
      dan value-nya adalah bulan saat kondisi itu pertama terdeteksi.

Lifecycle:
  new realm Early -> reset 0
  Early/Middle/Late but EXP not enough -> 0
  Late + enough EXP -> set to current month
  remains non-zero while waiting major breakthrough
  successful major breakthrough -> new realm Early -> reset to 0 at System phase

This is an ideal native state for making Action1031 state-aware.

Confidence: VERY HIGH / binary-confirmed.

=======================================================================
5. MONTHLY EVENT ORDER — IMPORTANT ONE-CYCLE NUANCE
=======================================================================

WorldRunOrder enum mapping:
  None
  Start
  NPC
  SystemStart
  System
  SystemEnd
  SystemEnd1
  Map
  MapEnd
  UnitEffect
  End

WorldRunMgr.On:
  0x052289CC

WorldUnitAIMgr.Init registration:
  0x0523C734 MOV W1,#2
  0x0523C740 BL WorldRunMgr.On
  => order 2 = NPC

WorldUnitAIDataBase.Init registration:
  0x05609D4C MOV W1,#4
  0x05609D58 BL WorldRunMgr.On
  => order 4 = System

Thus:
  NPC AI phase runs BEFORE the System phase that refreshes/reset fullExpMonth.

Implication for future state-aware Action1031:
  - if cultivation during NPC phase makes Late NPC hit full EXP this month,
    fullExpMonth is set later in the same month's System phase.
  - therefore cultivation 60000 may still be allowed for that NPC phase.
  - starting next monthly NPC phase, fullExpMonth != 0 and the 60000 override
    can safely be suppressed.

After breakthrough:
  old nonzero fullExpMonth may remain until System resets it because new realm is Early.
  This only prevents extra forced cultivation during the remainder of that same cycle;
  it does not block the next month permanently.

Expected additional delay from using this native state:
  at most approximately one monthly cycle.

This is safer than reimplementing EXP/phase eligibility inside the custom helper.

Confidence: VERY HIGH.

=======================================================================
6. GOAL101 — PHASE/UPGRADE PATH
=======================================================================

WorldUnitAIGoal101:
  Init             0x055C0888
  IsCreateGoal     0x055C0890
  UpdateWeightAdd  0x055C0944
  GoalStart        0x055C14E8
  UpGrade          0x055C1518

Important observations:
  - IsCreateGoal retains native grade/config validity checks.
  - UpdateWeightAdd calls ConfRoleGrade.IsUpGrade at 0x055C0A84.
  - V58 x20 hook is at 0x055C14BC, after native eligibility/calculation path.
  - GoalStart -> UpGrade remains native.
  - No direct Realm/Grade write is injected by V58.

Therefore V58 Goal101 x20 changes competition/weight only after native conditions
are satisfied; it does not force an illegal phase/realm transition.

Confidence: HIGH.

=======================================================================
7. GOAL141 — MAJOR BREAKTHROUGH NATIVE GATES
=======================================================================

WorldUnitAIGoal141.IsCreateGoal:
  0x055FC488

Exact gates observed:
  requester grade -> WorldUnitDynData.GetGrade

  0x055FC518 get minGrade
  reject if currentGrade < minGrade

  0x055FC550 get maxGrade
  reject if currentGrade >= maxGrade

  current ConfRoleGradeItem:
  0x055FC5AC LDR phase
  0x055FC5B0 CMP phase,#3

  if phase == Late:
    obtains player grade
    0x055FC5F4 CMP NPCgrade,PlayerGrade
    0x055FC5F8 B.GT reject

Thus a Late NPC cannot use this Goal to progress above player grade.
For the user's lagging Qi/Foundation NPCs this is not the observed bottleneck.

Then native cooldown markers:
  0x055FC628 get cdFateId
  0x055FC638 WorldUnitBase.GetLuck
  marker present => reject

  0x055FC67C get targetCdFateId
  0x055FC68C WorldUnitBase.GetLuck
  marker present => reject

Important:
  Goal141 does NOT require fullExpMonth merely to become a seek-material Goal.
  It can become active in Late phase and pursue requirements before final EXP-ready state,
  subject to native config/cooldown gates.

This makes fullExpMonth useful specifically as the point where forced cultivation
should stop competing so aggressively.

Confidence: VERY HIGH.

=======================================================================
8. seekGradeNeedNum = 1 — MATERIAL FALLBACK, BUKAN SEARCH TIMER
=======================================================================

Existing deep mapping remains valid:
  failed UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade -> seekGradeNum +1
  compare against seekGradeNeedNum
  when threshold reached -> AddUpGradeProps

AddUpGradeProps:
  - derives required breakthrough material IDs from native config
  - checks missing requirements
  - creates/gives exact missing requirement through native reward path
  - then native UnitActionRoleUpGrade can proceed

V60 effective Player-Sect threshold = 1.

Therefore:
  material acquisition is much less likely to be the PRIMARY reason a lagging
  NPC remains Foundation Late for many months, provided Goal141 actually gets
  an opportunity to run and fail once.

Do not reinterpret seekGradeNeedNum as "start searching after 1 month".

Confidence: VERY HIGH.

=======================================================================
9. FINAL ROOT-CAUSE RANKING FOR CURRENT V60
=======================================================================

1) Realm-gap monthly Goal budget penalty
   Confidence: VERY HIGH
   Effect: directly removes NPC AI opportunities when NPC falls behind player.

2) Action1031 cultivation weight 60000 competing inside a reduced budget
   Confidence: HIGH
   Effect: can consume scarce AI opportunities even when breakthrough should become priority.

3) Native Goal141 Fate/Luck cooldown eligibility
   Confidence: VERY HIGH that gate exists; runtime frequency remains config-driven.

4) Goal selection/top-weight limit (npcGoalMaxWeightCount)
   Confidence: HIGH
   Effect: second-stage selection bottleneck.

5) Breakthrough material acquisition
   Confidence as primary bottleneck: LOW-MEDIUM in current V60,
   because seekGradeNeedNum=1 enables native anti-stuck fallback quickly after failed seek.

6) Cultivation EXP multiplier too small
   Confidence as primary bottleneck: LOW.
   Current x20/x30/x40/x50 is already very large; frequency/opportunity is the larger issue.

=======================================================================
10. RECOMMENDED V61 NPC ARCHITECTURE — NOT APPLIED
=======================================================================

A) REMOVE ONLY REALM-GAP INACTIVITY PENALTY FOR PLAYER-SECT NPC
--------------------------------------------------------------
Candidate site:
  VA 0x0560A5E8 / file 0x056065E8

Concept:
  if requester is Player-Sect NPC:
      penalty quotient W8 = 0
  else:
      W8 = native SDIV W20,W22

Keep native:
  npcGoalMin
  npcGoalMinClamp
  random count
  weighted goal selection
  action creation
  all non-player-Sect NPC behavior

Risk level: GREEN candidate / high confidence.

B) MAKE ACTION1031 60000 STATE-AWARE
------------------------------------
Current logic conceptually:
  if Player-Sect NPC AND >=1 month since training:
      weight = 60000

Recommended:
  nativeWeight = native formula
  if monthsSinceTraining <= 0:
      native
  if not Player-Sect NPC:
      native
  if fullExpMonth != 0:
      native                         ; already major-breakthrough EXP-ready
  else:
      weight = 60000                ; still needs cultivation

Safe native data route available:
  Action1031 -> WorldUnitAIBase -> WorldUnitAIDataBase -> get_data 0x05600B0C
  -> Data +0x10 fullExpMonth

Why prefer this over direct ConfRoleGrade.IsUpGrade call in custom helper:
  - less invasive
  - uses a state already maintained by native game lifecycle
  - avoids custom reimplementation of EXP + phase + next-grade config
  - one-cycle delay is understood and benign.

Current Action1031 helper 0x08C78680..0x08C786BB is already tightly packed.
Future implementation will require a small separate helper/trampoline or expansion,
not an unsafe in-place overwrite. No code is changed during this report.

Risk level: GREEN concept / implementation layout still to be designed before patching.

=======================================================================
11. WHAT SHOULD REMAIN NATIVE
=======================================================================

Do not change for this fix:
  - Realm/Grade writes
  - ConfRoleGrade.IsUpGrade
  - Goal141 min/max grade
  - required breakthrough material IDs
  - CostPropItem
  - UnitActionRoleUpGrade
  - Goal141 cdFateId / targetCdFateId initially
  - family/friend/enemy routes
  - NPCs outside Player Sect

Reason:
  Current problem is opportunity/scheduling priority, not missing raw progression power.

=======================================================================
12. REMAINING UNKNOWN / RUNTIME TEST ITEMS
=======================================================================

The binary logic is now mapped enough to design V61 NPC fix.
Remaining items are not blockers for architecture, but should be measured/tested:

1) Actual config numeric values for npcGoalMin, npcGoalSub, npcGoalMinClamp
   are data/config-driven; this lib mapping proves formula but not the live data values.

2) Exact duration values behind Goal141 cdFateId/targetCdFateId are config-driven.
   They should remain native for the first V61 test.

3) After future patch, runtime test should record several NPCs for ~6-12 months:
   - realm/phase each month
   - whether they cultivate
   - whether they enter Late + full EXP
   - months until major breakthrough
   This will distinguish scheduler improvement from remaining Fate/Luck delays.

4) Do not increase EXP further until the scheduler/state-aware test is complete.

=======================================================================
FINAL CONCLUSION
=======================================================================

The current V60 NPC progression problem is now best explained by AI scheduling:

  player advances realms
      -> lagging sect NPC suffers realm-gap Goal-budget penalty
      -> fewer monthly Goal opportunities
      -> Action1031 cultivation can receive weight 60000
      -> Goal101/Goal141 compete for a smaller opportunity budget
      -> NPC can remain Middle/Late for much longer than expected despite huge EXP multipliers.

The native game already provides the exact state needed to stop over-cultivation:

  fullExpMonth (Data +0x10)

It is set only when the NPC is Late and has enough EXP for the next realm,
and reset when the NPC enters a new realm's Early phase.

Therefore the cleanest V61 strategy is NOT x100 EXP or forced Realm writes.
It is:
  1) remove realm-gap inactivity penalty for Player-Sect NPCs;
  2) stop forcing Action1031=60000 once fullExpMonth is non-zero;
  3) leave native breakthrough eligibility/material/cost/realm transition intact.

STATUS AT END OF REPORT:
  MAPPING COMPLETE ENOUGH FOR V61 NPC FIX DESIGN.
  NO PATCH HAS BEEN APPLIED.

=============================================================================
Tanggal audit: 2026-08-31
Status: MAPPING / PROTOTYPE ONLY — LIB V60 TIDAK DIUBAH
Fokus: Player-Sect NPC monthly AI goal budget + Action1031 cultivation stop-state

  c8c8e7ba9f4480d520c933e81c703f4286bb6a81e8528c1a1b2886168dcece06

=============================================================================
1. MONTHLY GOAL BUDGET — EXACT PATCH CONTEXT
=============================================================================

WorldUnitAIBase.RunUnit:
  VA 0x0560A15C

At function entry:
  X19 = WorldUnitAIBase this

Confirmed requester chain in this function:
  [X19 + 0x10] = requester WorldUnit
  requester +0x68 -> runtime unit state used by native GetGrade path

Core native realm-gap calculation:
  0x0560A55C  SUB  W20,W22,W23
  0x0560A57C  CNEG W20,W20,MI
  0x0560A598  get npcGoalSub
  0x0560A5A4  MOV  W22,W0
  0x0560A5E8  SDIV W8,W20,W22
  0x0560A5F0  SUB  W20,W21,W8
  0x0560A5F4  get npcGoalMinClamp
  0x0560A604  CSEL W21,W20,W0,GT

Thus original effective penalty quotient at hook site is:
  vanillaPenalty = floor(abs(NPCgrade-Playergrade)/npcGoalSub)

Patch candidate:
  VA          0x0560A5E8
  File        0x056065E8
  Original    88 0E D6 1A
  Instruction SDIV W8,W20,W22

=============================================================================
2. USER TARGET 7-10 — SAFEST DESIGN FOUND
=============================================================================

Requested behavior:
  - Player-Sect NPC around 7-10 monthly AI Goal opportunities
  - realm-gap penalty reduced, not completely removed
  - NPCs outside Player Sect remain vanilla

Safest design without patching global ConfGameParameter getters:

  if NOT Player-Sect NPC:
      W8  = vanilla SDIV penalty
      W21 = native npcGoalMin

  if Player-Sect NPC:
      nativePenalty  = floor(abs(gap)/npcGoalSub)
      reducedPenalty = floor(nativePenalty/2)
      reducedPenalty = min(reducedPenalty, 3)
      baseGoal        = 10

      effective count before native minClamp:
        10 - reducedPenalty

This yields:
  reducedPenalty 0 -> 10 goals
  reducedPenalty 1 ->  9 goals
  reducedPenalty 2 ->  8 goals
  reducedPenalty 3 ->  7 goals

So final target is naturally 7..10, with realm gap still having an effect but much weaker.

Why this is preferable to adding a new custom RNG 7..10:
  - no extra RNG call / managed object dependency
  - keeps native SUB, clamp and downstream scheduling
  - realm-gap penalty still matters as requested
  - only one caller-local hook is required
  - no global npcGoalMin/npcGoalSub config edits

The native minClamp remains in place. In normal vanilla config it is below this 7..10
range; no global clamp getter needs modification.

=============================================================================
3. npcGoalMaxWeightCount — DO NOT MODIFY IN FIRST BUILD
=============================================================================

Getter:
  0x032F9F78
Use:
  0x0560C970 -> value W24
  0x0560C99C -> passed into candidate/list selection routine

Deep disassembly of the callee shows list-count / candidate-selection behavior.
It is a second-stage weighted/candidate limiter, not proven to be the total monthly
execution-count field stored by RunUnit.

Therefore for the first 7-10 test:
  KEEP npcGoalMaxWeightCount VANILLA.

Reason:
  - raising monthly Goal budget and candidate diversity are separate mechanisms
  - changing both at once makes runtime diagnosis harder
  - current Goal101/Goal141 weights are already x20

If runtime later shows 7-10 slots but too little Goal diversity, this can be audited
separately.

=============================================================================
4. ACTION1031 — EXACT DATA CHAIN FOR fullExpMonth
=============================================================================

WorldUnitAIAction1031.UpdateWeightAdd:
  hook site VA 0x055AFA8C
  file      0x055ABA8C

Native context immediately before hook:
  0x055AFA44  LDR X8,[X19,#0x18]
  0x055AFA50  LDR X0,[X8,#0x18]
  0x055AFA5C  BL  0x05600B0C  ; AI data get_data
  0x055AFA64  LDR W22,[X0,#0x5C] ; lastTrainsMonst

Current V58/V60 helper:
  0x08C78680
  gives weight 60000 when currentMonth > lastTrainsMonst and requester is Player-Sect.

Exact Action1031 object chain available at hook:
  X19                = Action1031 object
  [X19 + 0x18]       = WorldUnitAIBase-like owner/context
  [owner + 0x10]     = requester WorldUnit
  [owner + 0x18]     = WorldUnitAIDataBase
  get_data 0x05600B0C
  [Data + 0x10]      = fullExpMonth

fullExpMonth semantics from prior deep audit:
  0 = not recorded as Late + enough EXP for next realm
  nonzero = native System phase has recorded major-breakthrough-ready EXP state
  resets to 0 when new realm enters Early phase

=============================================================================
5. ACTION1031 STATE-AWARE FINAL LOGIC
=============================================================================

Recommended replacement logic:

  nativeWeight = original MADD result

  if currentMonth <= lastTrainsMonst:
      return nativeWeight

  if requester is not Player-Sect NPC:
      return nativeWeight

  data = requester AI data
  if data is unavailable:
      return nativeWeight

  if data.fullExpMonth != 0:
      return nativeWeight

  return 60000

Meaning:
  - NPC still needs cultivation -> 60000 VERY HIGH remains
  - NPC is Late + EXP-ready -> 60000 stops automatically
  - Goal141 / other eligible progression Goals can compete normally
  - no direct EXP/Realm/Grade writes
  - native one-cycle timing of fullExpMonth is preserved

This is safer than calling ConfRoleGrade.IsUpGrade again inside the custom helper.

=============================================================================
6. CUSTOM RX PROTOTYPE LAYOUT — COMPILED/DISASSEMBLED, NOT APPLIED
=============================================================================

Current custom RX PT_LOAD:
  VA       0x08C78000
  File     0x08A40000
  Size     0x860
  End/EOF  0x08A40860

Prototype helper layout:

A) 7-10 scheduler helper
  VA 0x08C78860
  size 0x40 including alignment NOPs

Core behavior:
  save X0/LR
  requester = [X19+0x10]
  call existing is_player_school_npc 0x08C78080
  player Sect:
      SDIV vanilla penalty
      penalty >>= 1
      penalty = min(penalty,3)
      W21 = 10
  other NPC:
      replay native SDIV
  restore X0/LR
  return to 0x0560A5EC

Important ABI detail:
  X0 MUST be preserved because the native getter call at 0x0560A5F4 still relies on
  the config object already held in X0. Prototype explicitly preserves/restores X0.

B) state-aware Action1031 helper
  VA 0x08C788A0
  size 0x60

Core behavior:
  replay MADD
  preserve native W8 + LR
  existing month-gap test
  existing Player-Sect filter
  owner +0x18 -> AI data base
  call get_data 0x05600B0C
  read fullExpMonth +0x10
  nonzero -> native weight
  zero    -> 60000
  return 0x055AFA90

Total prototype extension:
  0xA0 bytes

If implemented, custom RX PT_LOAD would become:
  old size 0x860
  new size 0x900

No overlap found; current custom segment ends exactly at EOF and can be extended by 0xA0
with p_filesz/p_memsz updated together.

  CostPropItem
  UnitActionRoleUpGrade
  npcGoalMaxWeightCount initially
  all non-Player-Sect NPC scheduling

=============================================================================
10. RISK ASSESSMENT / TEST PLAN
=============================================================================

Scheduler 7-10 helper:
  risk: GREEN/YELLOW
  Main cost is runtime load: Player-Sect NPCs can process ~7-10 Goal opportunities
  instead of vanilla ~2-3-ish effective behavior. Next-Month processing may become slower
  if the current School contains many named NPCs.

Action1031 state-aware helper:
  risk: GREEN
  Uses native fullExpMonth; falls back to native weight whenever a pointer/state is missing.

First runtime test after eventual build should be 6-12 month skips and observe:
  - Next Month processing time
  - several Qi/Foundation NPC phases  - time to Late/fullExp
  - time from fullExp to major breakthrough
  - crash/soft-lock during monthly AI

If monthly processing is too heavy:
  lower base from 10 to 8/9 before reducing progression multipliers.

=============================================================================
FINAL MAPPING CONCLUSION
=============================================================================

For the user's requested 7-10 target, the cleanest first implementation is:

  Player-Sect NPC monthly Goal budget:
    base 10
    realm-gap penalty = half vanilla penalty
    penalty capped at 3
    => effective 7-10

  Action1031:
    60000 only while fullExpMonth == 0
    once native fullExpMonth becomes nonzero, use native cultivation weight

No need to accelerate calendar months, directly write realms, or raise Goal101/141
multipliers further.

1. RunUnit MONTHLY GOAL BUDGET — ORIGINAL MAP CONFIRMED
===================================================================================
WorldUnitAIBase.RunUnit:

Relevant native sequence confirmed:

  0x0560A5F0  SUB  W20,W21,W8
