IMPORTANT
---------
V17 is a correction/refinement pass over V14-V16.  No bytes in the current game library
were changed.  The most important correction is that the final RunUnit +0x80 loop is NOT
the final success gate for cultivation/breakthrough goals.  It checks an exact unit-effect
type: WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted.

=========================================================================================
1. V16 OUTER GOAL SCORE CAN NOW BE GIVEN ITS EXACT MANAGED FIELD NAMES
=========================================================================================

V16 mapped four selector bodies used by RunUnit:
  0x0560BA9C  float selector #1
  0x0560BAC8  int selector #1
  0x0560BAF0  float selector #2
  0x0560BB1C  int selector #2

All four compute:
  candidate +0x2C + [candidate+0x18] +0x28

Matching metadata resolves the object and fields as:

  candidate                  = WorldUnitAIGoalBase
  candidate +0x18            = WorldUnitAIGoalBase.goalBaseItem
  candidate +0x2C            = WorldUnitAIGoalBase.weightAdd
  goalBaseItem +0x28         = ConfNpcGoalBaseItem.weight

Therefore the exact effective outer-goal score is:

  effectiveGoalWeight = ConfNpcGoalBaseItem.weight + WorldUnitAIGoalBase.weightAdd

This score is used by the V15/V16 pipeline for:
  - first float sort,
  - npcGoalMaxWeightCount TOP-K preservation,
  - integer weighted selection,
  - second sort,
  - zero-weight RemoveAll filtering,
  - later integer weighted selection.

This upgrades the V16 selector semantics from structural to managed-name-confirmed.

Implication for Goal101:
  the existing Goal101 patch at 0x055C14BC modifies WorldUnitAIGoal101.weightAdd (+0x2C),
  so it directly raises this real native outer-goal selection score.

Confidence: VERY HIGH / metadata + machine code aligned.

=========================================================================================
2. EXACT GENERIC TYPE AT THE FORMER "FINAL +0x80 CANDIDATE" COLLECTOR
=========================================================================================

RunUnit closure <RunUnit>b__1 calls the generic collector at:
  call site : 0x0560CF28
  shared generic body: 0x06D4F7AC

The MethodInfo holder loaded at the call site is:
  holder VA: 0x08172498

The holder relocates to metadata-usage slot:
  VA: 0x08545308
  initial encoded usage value: 0xC0022B67

For this metadata format:
  0x22B67 = (0x115B3 * 2) + 1
  therefore MethodSpec index = 0x115B3

Binary MetadataRegistration was independently recovered.  Its methodSpecs table is:
  VA 0x01744968
  count 0x11676 / 71,286 entries

MethodSpec[0x115B3]:
  methodDefinitionIndex = 88652
  class generic inst    = -1
  method generic inst   = 7921

MethodDef 88652 resolves through V60 metadata to:
  WorldUnitBase.GetEffects<T>()
  token 0x06015A4D

GenericInst[7921] resolves to a single type argument:
  WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted

Adjacent MethodSpecs validate the decoding pattern:
  0x115B2 -> GetEffects<WorldUnitEffectSpecialBlaming>
  0x115B3 -> GetEffects<WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted>
  0x115B4 -> GetEffects<WorldUnitEffectSpecialBreakActionGridGetExp>
  0x115B5 -> GetEffects<WorldUnitEffectSpecialBreakActionGridMine>

Therefore the call at 0x0560CF28 is definitively:

  unit.GetEffects<WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted>()

It is NOT:
  - GetEffects of a goal/action candidate,
  - a list of cultivation/breakthrough goals,
  - a generic final progression-success list.

Confidence: VERY HIGH / MethodSpec + GenericInst + TypeDef decoded.

=========================================================================================
3. SOURCE REGISTRY +0xC0 IS EXACTLY WorldUnitBase._allWorldUnitEffectBaseCache
=========================================================================================

WorldUnitBase metadata contains:
  _allWorldUnitEffectBaseCache

Generated getter:
  WorldUnitBase.get_allWorldUnitEffectBaseCache @ 0x056401C0

Machine code is a direct:
  LDR X0,[X0,#0xC0]

Thus:
  WorldUnitBase._allWorldUnitEffectBaseCache runtime offset = +0xC0

RunUnit chain at 0x0560CF0C..0x0560CF28:
  closure +0x10      = <>c__DisplayClass19_0.<>4__this = WorldUnitAIBase
  WorldUnitAIBase+10 = unit
  unit               -> GetEffects<WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted>()

The generic shared body 0x06D4F7AC reads input +0xC0 and returns a new list containing
the matching effect subtype.  V15 already proved this helper preserves the matched source
collection enumeration order and does not sort/shuffle/normalize it.

Cache producer:
  WorldUnitBase.AddAllWorldUnitEffectBaseCache @ 0x05645450

Machine behavior of AddAllWorldUnitEffectBaseCache:
  - obtains the runtime type of the effect object,
  - looks up/creates a per-type list in _allWorldUnitEffectBaseCache,
  - appends the effect object to that list.

Thus +0xC0 is a type-indexed unit EFFECT cache, not a goal/action registry.

Confidence: VERY HIGH.

=========================================================================================
4. EXACT +0x80 / +0x88 TYPE AND FIELD NAMES IN THE RUNUNIT LOOP
=========================================================================================

WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted TypeDef:
  index 13861 / 0x3625

Own fields:
  rate
  key

Because WorldUnitEffectSpecial inherits the WorldUnitEffectBase/Special layout, its own fields
land at:
  +0x80 = rate (int)
  +0x88 = key  (managed reference/string-like key)

WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted.Create @ 0x05553958 proves both offsets directly:

  0x0555398C..0x05553990
      convert first configured value -> int
      STR W0,[X19,#0x80]
      => rate

  0x055539AC..0x055539B8
      load another configured value
      STR X1,[X19,#0x88] with GC write barrier
      => key

Therefore the RunUnit tail is exactly reading:
  effect.rate @ +0x80
  effect.key  @ +0x88

This also corrects V14's structural example at 0x0555EC98/0x0555ECE4.
That earlier writer belongs to WorldUnitEffectSpecialEquipSkill.Create, whose own fields happen
to occupy the same subclass offsets.  It is not the type consumed by RunUnit here.

Confidence: VERY HIGH.

=========================================================================================
5. EXACT MEANING OF THE Random(0,10000) LOOP
=========================================================================================

RunUnit <RunUnit>b__1:

  0x0560CF28  GetEffects<WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted>()
  0x0560CF30  list.Count

For every active BoutExhausted effect:
  0x0560CF6C  CommonTool.Random(0,10000)
  0x0560CF80  get effect[i]
  0x0560CF88  LDR W8,[effect,#0x80]  ; BoutExhausted.rate
  0x0560CF90  if random < rate -> 0x0560D59C

Random max is exclusive, so each draw is 0..9999.

For one active effect:
  P(trigger) = rate / 10000

For multiple independently checked BoutExhausted entries in list order:
  first passing effect wins;
  P(any triggers) = 1 - product(1 - rate_i/10000)

This is an ordered special-effect check, NOT the weighted goal picker mapped in V16.

Confidence: VERY HIGH.

=========================================================================================
6. BOUTEXHAUSTED PASS PATH SKIPS NORMAL CreateGoal FOR THAT CYCLE
=========================================================================================

The normal goal has already been selected before the effect check:
  0x0560CEE8  0x06C844A4 weighted-select from surviving goals
  0x0560CEF4  selected WorldUnitAIGoalBase stored in capture/wrapper
  0x0560CF00  selected goal null-check

Then BoutExhausted is checked.

A) NO BoutExhausted effect passes
---------------------------------
Execution falls through 0x0560CFA4 and continues through native run-log / goal preparation.
The normal goal path reaches:

  0x0560D3E0  WorldRunMgr.AddRunLog
  0x0560D440  WorldUnitAIBase.CreateGoal

Therefore failure of the BoutExhausted rate gate does NOT mean "the goal failed".
It means the selected normal goal is allowed to continue.

B) A BoutExhausted effect passes
--------------------------------
Execution jumps to 0x0560D59C.

The pass path:
  0x0560D5B8  CommonTool.SoleID(6)
  ...
  0x0560D634  LDR X2,[selectedEffect,#0x88]  ; BoutExhausted.key
  0x0560D64C  WorldUnitLogMgr.AddLogData
  0x0560D650..0x0560D664 invoke end/callback
  return

It does NOT reach the normal CreateGoal call at 0x0560D440 for that cycle.

Thus BoutExhausted is a native SPECIAL-EFFECT OVERRIDE/INTERRUPT layer placed AFTER a goal
has been weighted-selected but BEFORE that goal is actually created/executed.

This is the most important architectural correction in V17.

Confidence: VERY HIGH.

=========================================================================================
7. RELATIONSHIP TO THE AGGRESSIVE 19-ATTEMPT SCHEDULER
=========================================================================================

V14/V15 already proved the RunUnit attempt quota is decremented at:
  0x0560CE3C

The decrement occurs BEFORE:
  - the weighted goal selection at 0x0560CEE8,
  - the BoutExhausted check at 0x0560CF28..0x0560CF90.

Therefore an attempt can be consumed in two broad ways after a normal goal candidate was prepared:

  quota--
    -> weighted normal goal selected
       -> BoutExhausted does NOT trigger -> normal CreateGoal path
       OR
       -> BoutExhausted triggers         -> effect log/callback, normal CreateGoal skipped

This gives a much more precise explanation for why "19 attempts" is not equivalent to
"19 progression goal executions" even when progression weight is high.

However, V17 does NOT prove BoutExhausted is active frequently enough to be the dominant runtime
bottleneck.  Its source/rate distribution still needs to be measured/mapped before any patch is
justified.

=========================================================================================
8. IMPORTANT V14 CORRECTION — SECOND "GOAL101 RNG" WAS ACTUALLY GOAL102
=========================================================================================

V14 grouped two RNG sites under Goal101 because the stripped method boundary was not yet named.
Exact metadata corrects that.

A) TRUE Goal101 RNG
-------------------
  0x055C178C
  owner: WorldUnitAIGoal101.UpGrade

Threshold getter:
  0x038236B4
  exact name: ConfNpcGoal101Base.get_passSufferingProb

So the 0..99 draw is a real Goal101.UpGrade probability check against:
  passSufferingProb

B) NOT Goal101
--------------
  0x055C21D4
  owner: WorldUnitAIGoal102.UpdateWeightAdd

Threshold getter:
  0x03824BD8
  exact name: ConfNpcGoal102Base.get_lookBookBasicsProbability

Therefore the V14 statement that 0x055C21D4 is an internal Goal101 0..9999 RNG is wrong.
It belongs to Goal102 weighting logic.

This narrows the remaining Goal101-specific stochastic layer substantially.

Confidence: VERY HIGH.

The matching V60 metadata also corrects several labels that were intentionally approximate in

    not "Goal141 priority store" as a direct class owner.

These names make the RunUnit control flow much less ambiguous than in V14-V16.

=========================================================================================
10. CORRECTED RUNUNIT MODEL AFTER V17
=========================================================================================

A simplified but now semantically named model is:

  selected Player-Sect NPC
      |
      v
  attempt quota floor = max(19,native minimum)
      |
      v
  construct eligible WorldUnitAIGoalBase candidates
      |
      v
  effectiveGoalWeight = goalBaseItem.weight + weightAdd
      |
      +--> TOP-K via npcGoalMaxWeightCount
      +--> eligibility/filtering
      +--> weighted goal selection
      |
      v
  quota already consumed
      |
      v
  selected normal WorldUnitAIGoalBase
      |
      v
  unit.GetEffects<WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted>()
      |
      +--> no effect/rate pass
      |      -> normal run log
      |      -> WorldUnitAIBase.CreateGoal
      |
      +--> BoutExhausted rate pass
             -> WorldUnitLogMgr.AddLogData(effect.key)
             -> end callback
             -> selected normal goal is not created this cycle

Inside individual goals/actions there can still be their own native validation/RNG layers, e.g.
Goal101.UpGrade.passSufferingProb.

=========================================================================================
11. IMPLICATION FOR CURRENT PLAYER-SECT PROGRESSION MOD
=========================================================================================

The current progression multipliers are not disconnected from vanilla selection:
  - Goal101 weightAdd x20 feeds the real effective goal score.
  - Action1031 smart 60000 feeds a real action-layer weight path mapped in V16.
  - Goal141/CreateGoalInit and Action1051 remain separate layers to map more exactly.

But the old theory "large progression weight is lost because the final +0x80 gate fails" is false.
The +0x80 gate is specifically BoutExhausted.rate.

Therefore DO NOT patch:
  - all +0x80 fields globally,
  - all WorldUnitEffectSpecial subclasses,
  - CommonTool.Random globally,
  - scheduler quota above 19 merely to compensate,
  - Goal101's passSufferingProb until its gameplay consequence is fully classified.

A global +0x80=10000 patch would actually make BoutExhausted trigger constantly when the effect is
present, which is the opposite of the intended progression optimization.

  - identify which effect/config/fate/system installs WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted;
  - recover its configured `rate` values and `key` meanings;
  - determine how often ordinary Player-Sect NPCs can carry it;
  - decide whether it is a meaningful progression bottleneck or just a rare special state.

- Exact gameplay consequence of Goal101 passSufferingProb fail/pass state.
- Exact Goal141 <-> Action1051 linkage and action slot semantics.
- Exact Goal141 material bridge from CreateGoalInit local weight into selected action.
- Runtime frequency with which BoutExhausted steals Player-Sect progression attempts.

TALE OF IMMORTAL ANDROID ARM64 — V60 PLAYER-SECT NPC MAPPING V18
BOUTEXHAUSTED SOURCE / GOAL101 RESCUE / GOAL141→ACTION1051→SEEKUPGRADE PIPELINE
================================================================================
Date: 2026-09-02
Status: STATIC MAPPING ONLY — NO BINARY PATCH APPLIED

SOURCE
------
Current build:
  Tale_of_Immortal_ARM64_v60_SMART_PHASE_AGGRESSIVE_CATCHUP_FCFIX_V2-1

Current libil2cpp.so:
  SHA256: b115635f6ba4a4668823c6d7816c13752bf141964b4052c789ded4452be75c1f
  Size:   144,968,752 bytes
  Arch:   AArch64 Android IL2CPP

Exact matching V60 global-metadata.dat:
  SHA256: 9be3762476a87e8be23e3fa589b44f084027a5c7f1ff78b9be5f013fb5d131b3
  Metadata version: 31

  V17 proved that the RunUnit +0x80/+0x88 loop is specifically
  WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted processing, not the normal progression goal chooser.

================================================================================
1. V18 EXECUTIVE RESULT
================================================================================

V18 closes three major progression questions.

A) BoutExhausted is a real configured special effect.
   WorldFactory.CreateEffectSpecialBase recognizes the literal "boutExhausted" and creates
   WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted. Its special-effect payload is parsed into:
       values[0] -> rate (+0x80)
       values[1] -> key  (+0x88)
   Therefore the RunUnit interception chance comes from normal ConfRoleEffectItem special-effect
   configuration, not from Goal101/Goal141 weights.

B) Goal101.passSufferingProb is a rescue roll, not the normal breakthrough-success roll.
   UnitActionRoleUpGrade first computes state=1(success) or state=2(failure). Goal101.UpGrade only
   rolls passSufferingProb when state is already 2; a successful rescue changes state back to 1.
   In the current Player-Sect build, UnitActionRoleUpGrade.GetCompleteRate is already overridden to
   100 for exact current Player-Sect NPCs, so the ordinary 0..99 complete-rate roll cannot produce
   state=2. Thus passSufferingProb is normally moot for the mapped ordinary Player-Sect Upgrade path.

C) Goal141 -> Action1051 -> UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade is now a direct, metadata-named pipeline.
   Goal141 chooses a target route and target, serializes the target unitID into customActionData,
   Action1051.GetFixUnits resolves that fixed target, and Action1051's UnitActionToUnit closure
   directly constructs UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.

The most important remaining native failure layer is BEFORE the already-patched 100% dungeon gate:
UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.NPCToNPCAction performs a separate target/cooperation completion roll.
That first gate remains vanilla. Only if it passes does the current Player-Sect dungeon-success=100
helper guarantee the second gate.

However, the existing seekGradeNeedNum=1 patch supplies a strong anti-stuck fallback: on the first
counted failed seek in the mapped NPC-to-NPC path, native AddUpGradeProps can choose the configured
required material, create PropsData, reward it to the NPC, and continue into UnitActionRoleUpGrade.

================================================================================
2. BOUTEXHAUSTED — EXACT FACTORY / CONFIG INGRESSION
================================================================================

Factory:
  WorldFactory.CreateEffectSpecialBase @ 0x0565E9D4

Exact literal branch:
  around 0x05660D98, metadata string literal = "boutExhausted"

The matching branch allocates:
  WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted
and calls:
  WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted::.ctor @ 0x055539D0

General special-effect initialization:
  WorldUnitEffectSpecial.Init @ 0x0558F6AC

This method parses ConfRoleEffectItem special payload data and builds WorldUnitEffectSpecialData.
The metadata-defined special data contains:
  key
  values

BoutExhausted.Create uses the parsed values as:
  values[0] -> integer rate, stored at object +0x80
  values[1] -> string key, stored at object +0x88

This exactly matches V17's RunUnit consumer:
  Random(0,10000) < effect.rate
  on hit -> WorldUnitLogMgr.AddLogData(effect.key) + effect callback
  on no hit -> normal WorldUnitAIBase.CreateGoal path continues.

Creation ingress is through the normal effect system, including:
  WorldFactory.CreateEffect
  WorldUnitEffectTool.CreateEffect @ 0x05597204
  equipment/prop effect AddEffect paths.

CONCLUSION:
  BoutExhausted is not a hidden progression candidate. It is a configured special effect attached
  through the standard role/equipment/prop effect architecture.

YELLOW / UNRESOLVED:
  The exact production ConfRoleEffectItem rows containing "boutExhausted" and their numeric
  [rate,key] values are not present in the current mod ZIP. Matching metadata gives the schema and
  literal, but not the live config table rows. Therefore V18 deliberately does NOT invent a rate.

================================================================================
3. GOAL101.passSufferingProb — EXACT RESCUE SEMANTICS
================================================================================

Goal method:
  WorldUnitAIGoal101.UpGrade @ 0x055C1518

Ordinary upgrade action:
  UnitActionRoleUpGrade

Initial complete-state logic:
  Random(0,100)
  UnitActionRoleUpGrade.GetCompleteRate @ 0x055271B4
  + action.addCompleteRate
  comparison around 0x0552715C

State semantics from the native branch:
  state = 1 if random < completion threshold
  state = 2 if random >= completion threshold

Goal101.UpGrade then checks the action state.
Only when state==2 does it execute another Random(0,100) at:
  0x055C178C
and compare against:
  ConfNpcGoal101Base.passSufferingProb

If the rescue roll passes:
  action.state is changed from 2 back to 1.

Therefore passSufferingProb means approximately:
  "chance to pass/recover after suffering a failed ordinary breakthrough completion roll"
not:
  "the base breakthrough-success probability".

CURRENT PLAYER-SECT MOD EFFECT
------------------------------
Current hook:
  UnitActionRoleUpGrade.GetCompleteRate @ 0x055271B4 -> custom 0x08C78310

The helper:
  - checks exact current Player-Sect NPC via is_player_school_npc;
  - Player-Sect -> returns W0=100;
  - non-Player-Sect -> resumes vanilla continuation.

Because CommonTool.Random(0,100) yields 0..99, the ordinary mapped Player-Sect Upgrade roll with
threshold 100 always satisfies random < threshold. Thus it enters state=1, not state=2.

Practical result:
  For the ordinary UnitActionRoleUpGrade path, passSufferingProb is normally not the bottleneck for
  exact current Player-Sect NPCs.

CAVEAT:
  Special upgrade classes such as Rule/Elder variants must be mapped independently before claiming
  every special realm-transition path shares this exact success helper.

PATCH RECOMMENDATION:
  Do NOT patch passSufferingProb globally. It is already downstream of a Player-Sect 100% ordinary
  completion gate and is not the current primary progression blocker.

================================================================================
4. GOAL141 — FOUR TARGET ROUTES AND CURRENT GRID x20 PATCH
================================================================================

Goal:
  WorldUnitAIGoal141.CreateGoalInit @ 0x055FC6A0

Goal141 builds target pools and associates each non-empty pool with a configurable route weight.
Exact route mapping recovered from metadata string literals and getters:

  "0_" -> family route -> ConfNpcGoal141Base.familyWeight @ 0x0383F59C
  "1_" -> friend route -> ConfNpcGoal141Base.friendWeight @ 0x0383F5E8
  "2_" -> enemy route  -> ConfNpcGoal141Base.enemyWeight  @ 0x0383F634
  "3_" -> grid route   -> ConfNpcGoal141Base.gridWeight   @ 0x0383F680

Weighted route selection:
  CommonTool.RandomWeightString @ 0x0571EF80

Current Player-Sect grid patch:
  native gridWeight getter @ 0x055FD1CC
  hook                    @ 0x055FD1D0 -> 0x08C7872C

The helper is exact-current-Player-Sect scoped and multiplies the native gridWeight by 20.
Family/friend/enemy weights remain native.

IMPORTANT:
  x20 grid does not mean "always choose grid". If other non-empty pools have positive weights,
  they remain competitors in RandomWeightString.

After route selection, Goal141 selects one concrete target from the chosen route list.
Selected target store:
  Goal141.toUnit around 0x055FD320

Then the selected target is serialized to WorldUnitAIActionDataCommon:
  target WorldUnit -> WorldUnitData -> UnitInfoData -> unitID
  unitID copied into actionDataCommon.unitID around 0x055FD424
  actionDataCommon appended into WorldUnitAIGoalBase.customActionData.

This is the exact Goal141 -> downstream action target bridge.

================================================================================
5. ACTION1051 — EXACT GOAL141 WEIGHT + FIXED TARGET BRIDGE
================================================================================

Type:
  WorldUnitAIAction1051

Key methods:
  UpdateWeightAdd  @ 0x055BADA0
  CreateActionInit @ 0x055BB358
  UnitActionToUnit closure family begins near 0x055BB6F8

UpdateWeightAdd:
  calls ConfNpcGoal141.get_weight around 0x055BAE80
  current hook @ 0x055BAE84 -> custom 0x08C786F8

For exact current Player-Sect NPC:
  native Goal141 action weight is multiplied by 20.

Then vanilla Action1051 trait modifiers are still added from ConfNpcAction1051.
Therefore current Action1051 effective weight is not simply native*20 in every case; it is roughly:
  Player-Sect boosted Goal141 base + native matching trait additions.

CreateActionInit:
  calls WorldUnitAITool.GetFixUnits @ 0x0543F09C
  stores returned fixed target into Action1051.toUnit.

GetFixUnits consumes the fixed action data / actionDataCommon unitID created by Goal141 and resolves
that unitID through WorldUnitMgr.GetUnit.

Most decisive direct edge:
  Action1051 <>c__DisplayClass4_0.<UnitActionToUnit>b__0 @ 0x055BB6F8
  direct call @ 0x055BBF34 -> UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade::.ctor @ 0x0550BFCC

Arguments at that constructor include:
  target WorldUnit
  targetGradeId
  targetPropId
  isUpGrade flag

Therefore the path is now GREEN/direct:

  Goal141 target selection
      -> WorldUnitAIActionDataCommon.unitID
      -> Goal141.customActionData
      -> Action1051.GetFixUnits
      -> Action1051.toUnit
      -> Action1051 UnitActionToUnit
      -> UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade

================================================================================
6. UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade — TWO DISTINCT SUCCESS GATES
================================================================================
Method:
  UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.NPCToNPCAction @ 0x0550D7D4

Relevant state fields in this action family:
  isTargetComplete
  isDungeonComplete
  plus robbery/grade/target-material context fields.

FIRST GATE — TARGET / COOPERATION COMPLETION
-------------------------------------------
The first threshold starts from:
  ConfNpcGoal141Base.okBaseRate

The routine also applies native context modifiers involving multiple families, including:
  npcGradeRate1/2/3
  itemGradeRate1/2/3
  beautyRate1..7
  intimAddRate
  familyAddRate
  Action1051 trait configuration

First draw:
  Random(0,100) @ 0x0550E290

Result store:
  isTargetComplete @ 0x0550E29C

If this first gate fails, the path exits before reaching the dungeon-success gate.

SECOND GATE — DUNGEON COMPLETION
--------------------------------
Second draw:
  Random(0,100) @ 0x0550E2C4

Vanilla chooses a threshold from:
  dungeonHighSuccessRate
or:
  dungeonSuccessRate
based on native grade/context logic.

Current Player-Sect hook:
  0x0550E378 -> 0x08C78770

For exact current Player-Sect NPC the helper forces the chosen dungeon threshold to 100.
Result store:
  isDungeonComplete @ 0x0550E380

CORRECT INTERPRETATION OF EXISTING "DUNGEON SUCCESS 100%" PATCH:
  It guarantees the SECOND gate once the code reaches it.
  It does NOT guarantee the FIRST target/cooperation gate.

This is a real remaining vanilla failure layer and explains why an Action1051 seek can still fail
before the dungeon-success patch is even consulted.

================================================================================
7. seekGradeNeedNum=1 — NATIVE ANTI-STUCK FALLBACK AFTER FAILED SEEK
================================================================================

OnEnd callback:
  UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.<OnEnd>b__17_0 @ 0x055105C0

The mapped NPC-to-NPC progression path maintains a seek-failure counter in WorldUnitAIDataBase.
It increments the counter and reads:
  ConfNpcGoal141Base.seekGradeNeedNum @ 0x0383F7FC

Current hook:
  0x05510854 -> 0x08C7879C

Exact current Player-Sect behavior:
  threshold = 1

Native comparison semantics mean that when the new counted failure count reaches 1, the fallback is
already allowed to run; it is not waiting for a second failure.

Fallback helper:
  UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.<OnEnd>g__AddUpGradeProps|17_1 @ 0x055109C4

This helper is significant. It does not merely flip a boolean. It uses native configuration to:
  - obtain the required rarity/material Props ID;
  - create a PropsData object;
  - call WorldUnitData.RewardPropItem;
  - continue toward UnitActionRoleUpGrade.

Representative calls:
  PropsData.NewProps     @ call site 0x05510C2C
  RewardPropItem        @ call site 0x05510C44

The OnEnd path then creates UnitActionRoleUpGrade and can add the configured
ConfNpcAction1038Base.succeedProb to addCompleteRate.

Combined with the current exact-Player-Sect ordinary GetCompleteRate=100 helper, this creates a
strong convergence mechanism:
  failed seek -> first counted failure -> native required-material fallback -> ordinary upgrade
  with 100 completion rate.

CAVEAT:
  The fallback is reached through native eligibility/context branches. V18 does not claim every
  possible alternate/player-involved seek route always awards a material after one failure.

================================================================================
8. REVISED BOTTLENECK HIERARCHY AFTER V18
================================================================================

The current progression pipeline is best modeled as:

  (1) Outer goal competition
      effective weight = ConfNpcGoalBaseItem.weight + goal.weightAdd
      Goal101/Goal141 boosts already feed this native weighted selection.

  (2) BoutExhausted special-effect interception
      if the NPC currently has WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted and its configured rate rolls,
      the selected normal goal can be skipped for this attempt.

  (3) Goal141 route choice
      family / friend / enemy / grid weighted pools
      Player-Sect gridWeight is x20, not exclusive.

  (4) Action1051 selection
      Player-Sect Goal141-derived Action1051 base weight x20 + vanilla trait modifiers.

  (5) UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade NPC-to-NPC first gate
      target/cooperation completion remains native and can fail.

  (6) Dungeon gate
      exact Player-Sect threshold is forced to 100 once reached.

  (7) Failed-seek anti-stuck
      seekGradeNeedNum=1 allows native required-material fallback after the first counted failure
      in the mapped route.

  (8) Ordinary UnitActionRoleUpGrade
      exact Player-Sect GetCompleteRate=100, making Goal101 passSuffering rescue usually irrelevant.

Thus breakthrough-completion RNG itself is no longer the strongest suspect.
Remaining variability is more likely to come from:
  - whether BoutExhausted is actually attached, and its live config rate;
  - route/target eligibility before Action1051 reaches the mapped seek path;
  - the first NPC-to-NPC target completion gate;
  - alternate action/upgrade classes not yet mapped.

================================================================================
9. WHAT NOT TO PATCH AFTER V18
================================================================================

DO NOT:
- increase RunUnit quota above 19 simply to hide failures;
- force BoutExhausted.rate (+0x80) to 10000;
- globally delete/disable special effects without knowing which configs intentionally use them;
- patch Goal101.passSufferingProb to 100;
- direct-write Realm/Grade;
- force family/friend/enemy Goal141 weights to zero without mapping gameplay consequences;
- globally force the first target-completion gate to 100 before alternate routes are classified.

Why:
- BoutExhausted is a genuine special-effect mechanic, not a progression weight.
- passSuffering is already a rescue behind a 100% ordinary Player-Sect completion gate.
- seekGradeNeedNum=1 already provides a targeted native fallback after failed material seeking.
- broad patches could remove intended social/relationship/action behavior and create new AI loops.

================================================================================
10. BEST NEXT MAPPING TARGET — V19
================================================================================

Priority 1 — exact BoutExhausted config rows
  Recover the matching ConfRoleEffectItem asset/config rows and enumerate every
  boutExhausted [rate,key] source. Determine what equipment/prop/state attaches each one.

Priority 2 — Goal141 action alternatives
  Map ConfNpcGoalBaseItem.actions for Goal141 and classify Action1052/1053/1054/1055 or other
  alternate routes, instead of assuming every Goal141 execution becomes Action1051.

Priority 3 — failed-seek counter coverage
  Prove exactly which first-gate/dungeon/alternate-route failures increment the +0x158 seek counter
  and which bypass the AddUpGradeProps fallback.

Priority 4 — special upgrade classes
  Map UnitActionRoleUpGradeRule / UnitActionRoleUpGradeElder and verify whether their success logic
  uses the same Player-Sect 100% completion helper or a separate probability path.

Only after those are closed should a new patch be designed.

TALE OF IMMORTAL ANDROID ARM64 V60 — PLAYER-SECT NPC MAPPING V19
BOUTEXHAUSTED RATE / ACTION1052-1055 CLASSIFICATION / SEEK COUNTER CORRECTION / SPECIAL UPGRADE AUDIT
====================================================================================================

Date: 2026-09-02
Status: STATIC MAPPING ONLY — NO BINARY PATCH APPLIED

SOURCE
------
Current build:
  Tale_of_Immortal_ARM64_v60_SMART_PHASE_AGGRESSIVE_CATCHUP_FCFIX_V2-1

Current libil2cpp.so:
  SHA256: b115635f6ba4a4668823c6d7816c13752bf141964b4052c789ded4452be75c1f
  Size:   144,968,752 bytes
  Arch:   AArch64 Android IL2CPP

Matching V60 global-metadata.dat:
  SHA256: 9be3762476a87e8be23e3fa589b44f084027a5c7f1ff78b9be5f013fb5d131b3
  Metadata version: 31

Continuation:
  V17 proved that the late RunUnit Random(0,10000)<+0x80 loop is specifically
  WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted processing.

  a first target/cooperation gate before the already-patched dungeon-success gate.

====================================================================================================
1. V19 EXECUTIVE RESULT
====================================================================================================

V19 closes four important questions and corrects one important V18 interpretation.

A) V60 contains exactly four BoutExhausted payload literals in its metadata literal pool:
     boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90010022
     boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90020011
     boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90020021
     boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90020031

   WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted.Create already proved the parser semantics:
     values[0] -> rate
     values[1] -> key

   Therefore every one of these four payload variants encodes:
     rate = 5000
   and RunUnit interprets that as:
     Random(0,10000) < 5000
     => 50% trigger chance PER ACTIVE BoutExhausted effect, per checked goal attempt.

B) Action1052/1053/1054/1055 are NOT hidden alternate branches of Goal141.

   Metadata + method-RVA mapping gives:
     Goal141 -> Action1051 -> UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade
     Goal142 -> Action1052 -> UnitActionRoleHelpSeekProp
     Action1053 -> UnitActionRoleJealous
     Action1054 -> UnitActionRoleTeachSkill
     Action1055 -> Goal145-style reward/item logic

   Thus the major-breakthrough material path mapped through Action1051 is the correct direct family.

C) MAJOR V18 CORRECTION:
   seekGradeNeedNum is NOT a "failed seek count" threshold.

   UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.OnEnd only constructs/invokes its progression callback after:
     isTargetComplete == true
     AND
     isDungeonComplete == true.

   The callback then increments WorldUnitAIDataBase +0x158 for ordinary NPC->NPC isUpGrade seeks.

   So +0x158 is best described as a SUCCESSFUL-SEEK progression count for this mapped path.
   First-gate failures do NOT increment it.

   Current exact Player-Sect seekGradeNeedNum=1 therefore means:
     after the FIRST qualifying successful seek, native AddUpGradeProps is already eligible.

D) This moves the strongest remaining delay one step earlier:
   UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.NPCToNPCAction FIRST target/cooperation gate.

   If that first gate fails:
     - isTargetComplete=false;
     - dungeon-success=100 is never reached;
     - +0x158 does not advance;
     - seekGradeNeedNum=1 cannot help on that attempt.

   Once the first gate succeeds:
     - Player-Sect dungeon threshold is forced to 100;
     - the success callback can increment +0x158 to 1;
     - native AddUpGradeProps can become eligible immediately.

E) UnitActionRoleUpGradeRule and UnitActionRoleUpGradeElder use separate special-upgrade logic.
   Neither audited class routes through ordinary UnitActionRoleUpGrade.GetCompleteRate.
   Therefore the current Player-Sect ordinary 100% completion helper must NOT be claimed to cover
   Rule/Elder special breakthrough classes.

====================================================================================================
2. BOUTEXHAUSTED — EXACT V60 PAYLOAD SET AND RATE
====================================================================================================

V18 mapped:
  WorldFactory.CreateEffectSpecialBase      @ 0x0565E9D4
  literal branch "boutExhausted"            ~ 0x05660D98
  WorldUnitEffectSpecialBoutExhausted.Create @ 0x05553958

Runtime fields:
  BoutExhausted.rate +0x80
  BoutExhausted.key  +0x88

Create semantics:
  configured special values[0] -> integer rate
  configured special values[1] -> log key

V19 directly parsed the V60 metadata string-literal table and found exactly four literals beginning
with "boutExhausted_":

  #155215  boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90010022
  #155216  boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90020011
  #155217  boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90020021
  #155218  boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90020031

The matching standalone log-key literals also exist:
  exhaustedLog90010022
  exhaustedLog90020011
  exhaustedLog90020021
  exhaustedLog90020031

Since RunUnit uses:
  Random(0,10000)
and integer max is exclusive:
  random = 0..9999

For one active one of these effects:
  P(BoutExhausted intercept) = 5000 / 10000 = 0.5 = 50%.

For N simultaneously active matching effects:
  P(any intercept) = 1 - product_i(1 - rate_i/10000)

If all N use the observed 5000 rate:
  N=1 -> 50%
  N=2 -> 75%
  N=3 -> 87.5%
  N=4 -> 93.75%

IMPORTANT:
This does NOT mean every NPC has a permanent 50% skip chance.
The effect must first be attached/active on that WorldUnit.
V19 proves the configured payload values, not the runtime prevalence of the effect.

====================================================================================================
3. BOUTEXHAUSTED SOURCE FAMILY — ROLE FEATURE CORRELATION
====================================================================================================

The same V60 literal pool contains explicit postnatal role-feature localization keys:

  role_feature_postnatal_name9001002
  role_feature_postnatal_tips9001002

  role_feature_postnatal_name9002001
  role_feature_postnatal_name9002002
  role_feature_postnatal_name9002003
  role_feature_postnatal_tips9002001
  role_feature_postnatal_tips9002002
  role_feature_postnatal_tips9002003

It also contains:
  90010021|90010022
  90020011|90020012
  9002001_1000|9002002_1000|9002003_1000

and feature-condition / clear-feature expressions referencing:
  feature_0_9002001
  feature_0_9002002
  feature_0_9002003
  clearFeature_9002001
  clearFeature_9002002
  clearFeature_9002003

The BoutExhausted log keys share the same numeric families:
  exhaustedLog90010022  <-> 9001002 family
  exhaustedLog90020011  <-> 9002001 family
  exhaustedLog90020021  <-> 9002002 family
  exhaustedLog90020031  <-> 9002003 family

This is strong naming/config-family correlation that BoutExhausted is associated with these
postnatal feature families.

However, the exact live ConfRoleCreateFeatureItem row mapping:
  feature ID -> effect ID -> ConfRoleEffectItem.value
has not been recovered from the current lib/metadata pair alone.

Therefore:
  GREEN: four exact BoutExhausted payload strings and rate=5000.
  GREEN: postnatal feature keys 9001002 and 9002001/2/3 exist.
  YELLOW/HIGH: exact one-to-one attachment source row for each payload.

Do NOT globally delete these features or special effects on the basis of naming alone.

====================================================================================================
4. ACTION1052-1055 — THEY ARE SEPARATE GOAL/ACTION FAMILIES
====================================================================================================

V18 left a cautious question whether Goal141 could select other nearby numbered action classes.
V19 closes that question.

A) WorldUnitAIAction1052
------------------------
Methods:
  UpdateWeightAdd  @ 0x055BC19C
  CreateActionInit @ 0x055BC630
  ActionStart      @ 0x055BC6E4
  UnitActionToUnit @ 0x055BC988

Its UpdateWeightAdd reads ConfNpcGoal142 configuration.
Its fixed-target path constructs:
  UnitActionRoleHelpSeekProp::.ctor @ 0x054D54A4

Conclusion:
  Action1052 belongs to the Goal142/help-seek-prop family.
  It is not an alternate Goal141 major-breakthrough material action.

B) WorldUnitAIAction1053
------------------------
Methods:
  UpdateWeightAdd  @ 0x055BCDC0
  CreateActionInit @ 0x055BD22C
  ActionStart      @ 0x055BD2A0
  UnitActionToUnit @ 0x055BD2D8

UnitActionToUnit constructs:
  UnitActionRoleJealous::.ctor @ 0x054E233C

Conclusion:
  social/jealousy behavior, unrelated to Goal141 material progression.

C) WorldUnitAIAction1054
------------------------
Methods:
  UpdateWeightAdd  @ 0x055BD810
  CreateActionInit @ 0x055BDC7C
  ActionStart      @ 0x055BDCF0
  UnitActionToUnit @ 0x055BDD14

The path uses:
  WorldUnitAIGoal144.GetAllSkill
and constructs:
  UnitActionRoleTeachSkill::.ctor @ 0x05518A54

Conclusion:
  Goal144/teach-skill family, unrelated to Goal141 material progression.

D) WorldUnitAIAction1055
------------------------
Methods:
  CreateActionInit @ 0x055BDFC8
  ActionStart      @ 0x055BDFD0
  .ctor            @ 0x055BE830

ActionStart reads ConfNpcGoal145 grade-weight/success configuration and performs item/reward/log logic.

Conclusion:
  Goal145-style reward/item behavior, not Goal141 seek-upgrade.

E) Correct Goal141 family
-------------------------
The direct V18 chain remains valid:

  WorldUnitAIGoal141
      -> WorldUnitAIAction1051
      -> UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade

No Action1052-1055 detour is needed to explain the mapped major-breakthrough material route.

====================================================================================================
5. seekGradeNeedNum — V18 FAILURE-COUNTER INTERPRETATION IS WRONG
====================================================================================================

Action:
  UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade

Important fields:
  target WorldUnit          inherited +0x38
  isTargetComplete          +0x9A
  isDungeonComplete         +0x9B
  isRob                     +0x9C
  isRobIdea                 +0x9D
  isRobComplete             +0x9E
  itemGrade                 +0xA0
  targetGradeId             +0xA4
  targetPropId              +0xA8
  isUpGrade                 +0xAC

OnEnd:
  UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.OnEnd @ 0x0550C334

Critical checks:
  0x0550C3BC  reads isTargetComplete
  0x0550C3D0  reads isDungeonComplete

Only when both are true does this path build the progression callback that reaches:
  UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.<OnEnd>b__17_0 @ 0x055105C0

Thus:
  first target gate fail  -> no progression callback
  dungeon gate fail       -> no normal successful-seek callback
  both complete           -> callback becomes reachable

Inside b__17_0, the ordinary pure-NPC path excludes player-involved cases and then obtains:
  WorldUnitAIDataBase

Counter operations:
  0x05510824  LDRB W8,[AIData,#0x158]
  0x0551082C  ADD  W22,W8,#1
  0x05510830  STRB W22,[AIData,#0x158]

Then:
  ConfNpcGoal141Base.seekGradeNeedNum is read
  current patch site 0x05510854 -> custom helper 0x08C7879C
  exact current Player-Sect threshold = 1

When the incremented counter reaches the threshold, native:
  UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.<OnEnd>g__AddUpGradeProps|17_1 @ 0x055109C4
can be called.

A later branch can reset the same counter:
  0x055109A8  STRB WZR,[AIData,#0x158]

CORRECT SEMANTIC DESCRIPTION:
  +0x158 is a successful qualifying seek count for this mapped NPC->NPC upgrade route.

It is NOT supported to call it:
  "number of failed seeks."

This corrects V18 sections that described:
  failed seek -> counter++ -> fallback.

The corrected model is:
  successful target cooperation
      -> successful dungeon
      -> successful-seek callback
      -> counter++
      -> if counter >= seekGradeNeedNum
           native AddUpGradeProps can supply required configured material.

With current Player-Sect threshold=1:
  ONE qualifying successful seek is sufficient.

====================================================================================================
6. WHY THE FIRST TARGET/COOPERATION GATE IS NOW THE MAIN MAPPED DELAY
====================================================================================================

UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.NPCToNPCAction @ 0x0550D7D4

FIRST gate:
  base:
    ConfNpcGoal141Base.okBaseRate

Native modifiers include:
  npcGradeRate1/2/3
  itemGradeRate1/2/3
  beautyRate1..7
  intimAddRate
  familyAddRate
  Action1051 trait/config context

First draw:
  Random(0,100) @ 0x0550E290

Result:
  isTargetComplete store @ 0x0550E29C

If random >= computed first threshold:
  the code leaves before the second/dungeon success path.

SECOND gate:
  Random(0,100) @ 0x0550E2C4

Current exact Player-Sect helper:
  hook site 0x0550E378 -> 0x08C78770
  selected dungeon threshold -> 100

Result:
  isDungeonComplete @ 0x0550E380

Therefore:

CASE A — first gate FAILS
  isTargetComplete = false
  -> dungeon 100% helper is irrelevant
  -> successful-seek callback not created/invoked
  -> +0x158 does not increment
  -> seekGradeNeedNum=1 does not activate AddUpGradeProps this attempt

CASE B — first gate PASSES
  -> second gate reached
  -> Player-Sect dungeon threshold = 100
  -> isDungeonComplete becomes true
  -> successful-seek callback can run
  -> +0x158 becomes 1
  -> threshold 1 allows AddUpGradeProps immediately

This makes the first target/cooperation RNG the strongest remaining RANDOM delay in the mapped
ordinary NPC->NPC Goal141 -> Action1051 material route.

This does NOT yet justify globally forcing it to 100, because its threshold intentionally contains
social/grade/beauty/relationship logic and the same action family may be used in contexts that
should remain native.

====================================================================================================
7. SPECIAL UPGRADE CLASSES — ORDINARY 100% COMPLETE HELPER DOES NOT COVER THEM
====================================================================================================

The existing Player-Sect 100% ordinary breakthrough helper hooks:
  UnitActionRoleUpGrade.GetCompleteRate @ 0x055271B4

That guarantee is valid for ordinary UnitActionRoleUpGrade.

V19 audited two separate subclasses/families.

A) UnitActionRoleUpGradeElder
-----------------------------
Relevant methods:
  IsPassElderDungeon @ 0x0552C30C
  OnCreate           @ 0x0552C3D0
  IsUpgradeElder     @ 0x0552D4E8

The audited path handles its own Elder eligibility/dungeon/config/material logic and ultimately calls:
  UnitActionRoleUpGrade.ActionUpGradeComplete @ call site 0x0552D32C

No call to ordinary:
  UnitActionRoleUpGrade.GetCompleteRate @ 0x055271B4
was found in the audited Elder path.

Conclusion:
  ordinary Player-Sect GetCompleteRate=100 must NOT be claimed to cover Elder upgrade semantics.

B) UnitActionRoleUpGradeRule
----------------------------
Relevant methods:
  IsRule   @ 0x0552F310 / 0x05530D2C
  OnCreate @ 0x0552F830

The Rule path contains its own Random calls, including:
  CommonTool.Random @ 0x055304A4

and ultimately:
  UnitActionRoleUpGrade.ActionUpGradeComplete @ call site 0x05530ACC

No direct call to ordinary UnitActionRoleUpGrade.GetCompleteRate was found in the audited Rule path.

The Random at 0x055304A4 is associated with Rule-specific config/effect handling and must NOT be
blindly labeled "breakthrough complete rate" without another semantic pass.

Conclusion:
  Rule is also a separate probability/eligibility family.

Practical scope correction:
  current "eligible breakthrough success 100%" is GREEN for ordinary UnitActionRoleUpGrade,
  but special Elder/Rule classes remain separate and should be mapped/treated independently.

====================================================================================================

====================================================================================================
9. PATCH IMPLICATION AFTER V19
====================================================================================================

DO NOT PATCH YET:
  - do not increase quota above 19;
  - do not force BoutExhausted.rate globally;
  - do not delete role features 9001002 / 9002001/2/3 globally;
  - do not change seekGradeNeedNum lower than 1 (1 is already immediate after first qualifying seek);
  - do not patch passSufferingProb;
  - do not globally force first target/cooperation threshold to 100;
  - do not claim Rule/Elder are already guaranteed by ordinary GetCompleteRate=100.

Why:
  - V19 shows seekGradeNeedNum is already as aggressive as useful: threshold 1.
  - failure before that counter is the first target gate, not "too many failed seeks required."
  - a broad first-gate patch would erase relationship/grade/beauty mechanics for unrelated NPCs.
  - BoutExhausted 50% is meaningful when active, but runtime attachment prevalence/source rows are not
    yet completely mapped.

If a future patch is justified, the safest candidate architecture is likely:
  exact current Player-Sect
  AND Action1051 / UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade
  AND isUpGrade=true
  AND pure NPC->NPC route
  -> selectively increase/min-floor only the FIRST target/cooperation threshold.

That is far narrower than changing CommonTool.Random, ConfNpcGoal141 globally, or all social actions.

====================================================================================================
10. BEST NEXT MAPPING TARGET — V20
====================================================================================================

Priority 1 — exact BoutExhausted attachment row/source
  Recover the live ConfRoleCreateFeatureItem -> effect -> ConfRoleEffectItem mapping for:
    9001002
    9002001
    9002002
    9002003
  and prove which exact role features create each 5000-rate BoutExhausted entry.

Priority 2 — first target-gate formula and safe scope
  Fully name each term contributing to W22 before 0x0550E290 and map min/max/range behavior.
  Determine whether a Player-Sect-only first-gate floor can be applied only when:
    UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.isUpGrade == true
    actor != player
    target != player
  without changing ask/help/social gameplay.

Priority 3 — runtime feature prevalence
  Identify how often normal generated Player-Sect NPCs can carry 9001002 / 9002001/2/3 and whether
  these features are temporary, permanent, mutually exclusive, or removable.

Priority 4 — Rule/Elder completion semantics
  Classify Rule Random branches and Elder dungeon eligibility precisely.
  Only then decide whether special upgrade classes need a separate Player-Sect success helper.

Priority 5 — successful-seek counter lifecycle
  Map all reset/decrement/clear sites for WorldUnitAIDataBase +0x158 and prove whether it survives
  months, failed attempts, target changes, and realm transitions.

11. V19 CONFIDENCE TABLE
====================================================================================================

GREEN / VERY HIGH
-----------------
- Matching V60 lib and metadata identity.
- Exactly four metadata literals beginning boutExhausted_.
- All four encode rate=5000.
- RunUnit interprets rate=5000 as 50% per active effect.
- Goal141 -> Action1051 -> UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade is the direct mapped family.
- Action1052 -> UnitActionRoleHelpSeekProp / Goal142 family.
- Action1053 -> UnitActionRoleJealous.
- Action1054 -> UnitActionRoleTeachSkill / Goal144 family.
- Action1055 uses Goal145-style reward logic.
- OnEnd checks isTargetComplete and isDungeonComplete before progression callback.
- Pure NPC->NPC successful callback increments AIData +0x158.
- seekGradeNeedNum=1 is compared AFTER that increment.
- First target-gate failure does not advance the successful-seek counter.
- Ordinary Player-Sect dungeon threshold remains 100 once second gate is reached.
- Elder/Rule audited paths do not call ordinary UnitActionRoleUpGrade.GetCompleteRate.

GREEN / HIGH
------------
- +0x158 is a successful qualifying seek count in the mapped ordinary NPC->NPC isUpGrade path.
- The first target/cooperation RNG is currently the strongest random delay in that mapped route.
- Rule/Elder need separate success analysis from ordinary UnitActionRoleUpGrade.

YELLOW / NEXT
-------------
- Exact live config row linking each postnatal feature ID to each BoutExhausted payload.
- Runtime prevalence and duration of BoutExhausted-carrying postnatal features.
- Exact gameplay meaning of every Rule-specific Random branch.
- Exact Elder special completion/dungeon probability semantics.
- Full lifecycle/reset semantics of AIData +0x158 beyond the mapped OnEnd path.

TALE OF IMMORTAL V60 — V18 TO V19 CORRECTIONS
====================================================

1) V18 wording "seekGradeNeedNum=1 = fallback after first counted failed seek" is WRONG.

V19 instruction-level control-flow proof:
- UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade.OnEnd checks isTargetComplete and isDungeonComplete.
- The progression callback <OnEnd>b__17_0 is reached in the mapped normal path only after both succeed.
- That callback increments WorldUnitAIDataBase +0x158.
- seekGradeNeedNum is compared after this increment.

Correct wording:
  +0x158 = successful qualifying seek count for the mapped NPC->NPC isUpGrade route.
  seekGradeNeedNum=1 = one qualifying successful seek is sufficient to enable native AddUpGradeProps.

A first target/cooperation failure does not increment this counter.

2) V18 uncertainty that Action1052-1055 might be alternate Goal141 actions is closed.

Correct families:
  Goal141 -> Action1051 -> UnitActionRoleSeekUpGrade
  Goal142 -> Action1052 -> UnitActionRoleHelpSeekProp
  Action1053 -> UnitActionRoleJealous
  Action1054 -> UnitActionRoleTeachSkill / Goal144 family
  Action1055 -> Goal145 reward/item logic

3) BoutExhausted numeric rate is no longer unknown at the literal-payload level.

V60 metadata contains exactly:
  boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90010022
  boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90020011
  boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90020021
  boutExhausted_5000_exhaustedLog90020031

All encode rate=5000 => 50% per active effect.
The remaining unknown is the exact live feature/effect-row attachment prevalence, not the payload rate itself.
